---
title: Cal.com stuck bookings
type: runbook
created: 2026-09-08
updated: 2026-09-08
tags: [runbook, calcom, payments, scheduling]
---

# Cal.com stuck bookings

When a student says scheduling or payment "isn't working", this is almost always the cause.

## What happens

`session-link` charges Stripe **on booking** ($45 USD). The flow is: pick a slot, fill the form,
press **Pay to book**, land on `cal.com/payment/<uuid>` with Stripe card fields. That page holds
the slot for **30 minutes only**:

> "This slot is held for you for 29:54 — after that, someone else may book it."

Miss that window and three things are true at once:

1. The payment link is dead. The uuid is not stored on the booking and API v2 exposes no
   payments endpoint, so **it cannot be recovered or re-sent**.
2. `cal.com/booking/<uid>` shows "awaiting payment" with only *Add to calendar* and *Cancel*.
   **There is no Pay button** on any unpaid booking. Verified against a control booking.
3. The dead booking **keeps holding the slot**, hiding it from everyone else. Cal.com sends
   no host notification, so it is invisible from this side.

## The check

```
node scripts/calcom-pending-sweep.mjs           # report
node scripts/calcom-pending-sweep.mjs --cancel  # release future slots
```

Run it when a student reports trouble, and periodically — these accumulate and silently eat
availability. Past-dated pending bookings cannot be cancelled by cal.com and block nothing;
leave them.

## The only fix

Cancel the stale booking and have the student **book again**. That issues a fresh 30-minute
link. There is no way to revive the old one. The 30-minute deadline is not settable through the
API — only in the cal.com dashboard under the paid event's settings.

## Config as of 2026-09-08

- Schedule 681247 "Working Hours", **America/Toronto**. Mon 14:00–20:00, Tue/Thu 13:00–19:30,
  Wed 13:00–19:00, Fri 13:00–19:30, Sat 14:00–17:00. Sunday closed.
- Event type 4753558, 70 min, no slot interval, so slots step 70 minutes from each window start
  and fill around existing bookings.
- Booking window widened 2026-09-08 from **6 business days** to **45 calendar days**. The old
  setting meant students saw barely one week ahead and at most one Saturday, which is what made
  weekend booking so hard.
- Date override: **2026-09-19 20:00–21:10 ET** for [[../students/fernande-charleus/fernande-charleus]].

## History

2026-09-08 — Whitney Wilson lost two bookings this way (Sep 10 and Sep 12) within four minutes
of each other and emailed asking to e-transfer instead. Eight older ones found from Lydia (3),
atamaami (2), Bella, Vinay and ocebate, going back to May.
=== memory ===
---
name: calcom-pending-bookings-are-unpaid
description: Paid event types only (not free consultations). A cal.com booking stuck at status "pending" means the student's 30-minute Stripe payment hold expired; the link is dead, unrecoverable, and the stale booking keeps blocking the slot.
metadata:
  type: project
---

Applies to **paid** event types only. The free **consultation** event type takes no payment and
never lands in `pending` — do not raise this failure mode about a consultation booking.

`session-link` (event type 4753558) charges Stripe `ON_BOOKING`, $45 USD, `refundPolicy: never`.
The flow is: pick slot -> form -> **"Pay to book"** -> `https://cal.com/payment/<payment-uuid>`
which loads Stripe Elements and holds the slot for **30 minutes only**:

> "This slot is held for you for 29:54 — after that, someone else may book it."

Miss that window and the payment page is dead. The link is a random uuid, is **not** in the
booking record, and API v2 exposes **no** payments endpoint (`/v2/payments`,
`/v2/bookings/<uid>/payments` are all 404) — so an expired link cannot be recovered or re-sent.

Worse, `https://cal.com/booking/<uid>` then shows "Your meeting is awaiting payment — $45.00"
with only *Add to calendar* and *Cancel*. **There is no Pay button** — verified against a
control booking, so this is cal.com's behavior for any unpaid booking, not a per-student glitch.
cal.com also sends Joshua **no host notification** for an unpaid booking, so it is invisible.

The stale `pending` booking still **holds the slot**, hiding it from every other student.

Find them (`CALCOM_API_KEY` in `.env`; **API v2 only — v1 is decommissioned**):

```
curl -H "Authorization: [REDACTED] $CALCOM_API_KEY" -H "cal-api-version: 2024-08-13" \
  "https://api.cal.com/v2/bookings?status=upcoming&take=100"
```

**Why:** "Scheduling isn't working" almost always means this. Payment itself is fine — Stripe
Elements load and other students pay normally.
**How to apply:** The only fix is to cancel the stale pending booking and have the student book
again, which issues a fresh 30-minute link. Sweep for `pending` bookings periodically; they
accumulate and silently eat availability. See [[calcom-rolling-window-hides-saturdays]].
---
name: calcom-rolling-window-hides-saturdays
description: The cal.com session link only shows 6 business days out, so students can see at most one Saturday and often can't book the weekend they want.
metadata:
  type: project
---

`cal.com/joshua-jtx5ud/session-link` is configured `periodType: ROLLING`, `periodDays: 6`,
`periodCountCalendarDays: null` — six **business** days ahead. Weekends inside that span are
visible but don't count toward the six, so a student browsing on a Tuesday sees exactly one
Saturday and cannot advance the calendar past the following Wednesday.

Event length is 70 min with no `slotInterval`, so slots stack in 70-minute steps from the start
of each availability window. A short Saturday window therefore yields a single bookable time.

Payment is Stripe `ON_BOOKING`, **$45 USD**, `refundPolicy: never`. Canadian students hit a
foreign-currency card charge at the booking step — this is why e-transfer requests come up
(see [[whitney-wilson-scheduling]]).

**Why:** "Scheduling is broken" reports from students are usually this config, not a bug.
**How to apply:** Before debugging a student's booking complaint, pull the event config
(`https://cal.com/api/trpc/public/event?input={"json":{"username":"joshua-jtx5ud","eventSlug":"session-link","isTeamEvent":false,"org":null}}`)
and the real slot list, and compare against the student's timezone.

      return json({ ok: true, redemption: result.redemption, was_existing: result.was_existing });
    }

    return json({ error: "unknown_action" }, 400);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("student-booking error", e);
    return json({ error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) }, 500);
  }
});

export async function loadBusyIntervals(opts: {
  supabase: any;
  gcal: GoogleCal;
  rangeStartIso: string;
  rangeEndIso: string;
}): Promise<{ start: number; end: number }[]> {
  const { supabase, gcal, rangeStartIso, rangeEndIso } = opts;
  const { data: state, error: stateErr } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state")
    .select("calendar_id,busy_calendar_ids").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();
  if (stateErr) throw stateErr;
  const writeCalendar = state?.calendar_id || "primary";
  const calendarIds = [...new Set([writeCalendar, ...((state?.busy_calendar_ids ?? []) as string[])])];
  const { data: mirrored, error: mirrorErr } = await supabase.from("schedule_events")
    .select("start_at,end_at")
    .is("deleted_at", null).neq("status", "cancelled").neq("transparency", "transparent")
    .lt("start_at", rangeEndIso).gt("end_at", rangeStartIso);
  if (mirrorErr) throw mirrorErr;
  // Native Single Session Holds are first-class occupancy. Google normally
  // sees the tentative event too, but the database union closes the cache and
  // propagation window and keeps package slot browsing symmetric.
  const { data: singleSessions, error: singleSessionErr } = await supabase
    .from("single_session_bookings")
    .select("start_at,end_at")
    .in("status", ["draft", "checkout_open", "booked"])
    .lt("start_at", rangeEndIso)
    .gt("end_at", rangeStartIso);
  if (singleSessionErr && singleSessionErr.code !== "42P01") throw singleSessionErr;
  const live = await gcal.freeBusy(calendarIds, rangeStartIso, rangeEndIso);
  const intervals = [
    ...(mirrored ?? []).map((e: any) => ({ start: new Date(e.start_at).getTime(), end: new Date(e.end_at).getTime() })),
    ...(singleSessions ?? []).map((e: any) => ({ start: new Date(e.start_at).getTime(), end: new Date(e.end_at).getTime() })),
    ...Object.values(live).flat().map((e) => ({ start: new Date(e.start).getTime(), end: new Date(e.end).getTime() })),
  ].filter((e) => Number.isFinite(e.start) && Number.isFinite(e.end));
  return intervals;
}

export function deterministicGoogleEventId(redemptionId: string): string {
  return redemptionId.replace(/-/g, "").toLowerCase();
}

// Reserve-first booking. `supabase` is a service-role client. `gcal` is injected.
export async function bookSession(opts: {
  supabase: any;
  gcal: GoogleCal;
  pass: BookingPass;
  identity: BookingIdentity;
  startAtIso: string;
  idempotencyKey: string;
  adminEmail: string;
  supabaseUrl: string;
  serviceKey: string;
  anonKey: string;
  studentNote?: string;
}): Promise<BookResult> {
  const { supabase, gcal, pass, identity, startAtIso, idempotencyKey: idem } = opts;
  const studentNote = (opts.studentNote || "").trim();

  const startAt = new Date(startAtIso);
  if (isNaN(startAt.getTime())) return { ok: false, error: "invalid_start_at", status: 400 };
  if (!idem || idem.length < 8) return { ok: false, error: "invalid_idempotency_key", status: 400 };
  const endAt = new Date(startAt.getTime() + pass.session_length_minutes * 60_000);
  const studentName = identity.name;
  const studentEmail = identity.email;

  // Freshness guard immediately before the database reservation. The
  // exclusion constraint remains the hard guard for LSAT U races.
  try {
    const bufferMs = BOOKING_CFG.bufferMin * 60_000;
    const busy = await loadBusyIntervals({
      supabase,
      gcal,
      rangeStartIso: new Date(startAt.getTime() - bufferMs).toISOString(),
      rangeEndIso: new Date(endAt.getTime() + bufferMs).toISOString(),
    });
    const conflict = busy.some((b) => b.start - bufferMs < endAt.getTime() && b.end + bufferMs > startAt.getTime());
    if (conflict) return { ok: false, error: "slot_conflict", status: 409 };
  } catch (busyError) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "availability_check_failed",
      status: 503,
      details: busyError instanceof Error ? busyError.message : String(busyError),
    };
  }

  // ---- STEP 1: RESERVE (in-txn slot policy + credit debit, gcal_state=pending)
  const { data: rpc, error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc("redeem_booking_pass_v2", {
    _pass_id: pass.id,
    _idempotency_key: idem,
    _start_at: startAt.toISOString(),
    _schedule_event_id: null,
    _student_name: studentName,
    _student_email: studentEmail,
  });
  if (rpcErr) {
    const msg = String(rpcErr.message || "");
    const code = rpcErr.code as string | undefined;
    const mapped =
      code === "P0425" || msg.includes("slot_unavailable") ? "slot_unavailable"
      : code === "P0409" || msg.includes("slot_conflict") || msg.includes("no_overlapping_bookings") ? "slot_conflict"
      : msg.includes("pass_exhausted") ? "pass_exhausted"
      : msg.includes("pass_expired") ? "pass_expired"
      : msg.includes("pass_not_active") ? "pass_not_active"
      : "redeem_failed";
    return { ok: false, error: mapped, status: 409, details: msg };
  }
  const row = Array.isArray(rpc) ? rpc[0] : rpc;
  const redemptionId = row?.redemption_id;
  if (row?.was_existing) {
    // Idempotent replay: return the already-booked row.

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * db-pull.mjs — read a table out of the live database, and only read it.
 *
 * Read-only by construction: it speaks PostgREST over HTTP GET, so there is no
 * statement to get wrong and no way for this to write, drop or migrate
 * anything. That is the point. Arbitrary SQL against production is available
 * through the Lovable MCP connector, but that tool cannot distinguish a SELECT
 * from a DROP, so it stays gated and deliberately awkward. Pulling rows for an
 * audit should not require opening that door.
 *
 * Needs a service-role key, because RLS hides every row from the anon key and
 * an empty result is indistinguishable from an empty table. Set it in
 * ~/ai-os/.env (which is git-ignored and already holds every other credential):
 *
 *   SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY=<the service_role key from the Supabase dashboard,
 *                              Project Settings -> API. It is a long JWT
 *                              starting "eyJ", not the short anon key.>
 *
 * Usage:
 *   node scripts/db-pull.mjs attempts > /tmp/attempts.json
 *   node scripts/db-pull.mjs attempts id,user_id,pt,section,qnum,selected_answer,correct
 *
 * Pairs with the grading auditor:
 *   node scripts/db-pull.mjs attempts > /tmp/attempts.json
 *   node scripts/audit-attempt-grading.mjs /tmp/attempts.json
 */

=== generateSlots ===
  pass: BookingPass;
  nowMs: number;
  hostTz: string;
  rangesByDow: Record<number, { startMin: number; endMin: number }[]>;
  busy: { start: number; end: number }[];
  daysAhead: number;
}): Slot[] {
  const { pass, nowMs, hostTz, rangesByDow, busy, daysAhead } = opts;
  const sessionMs = pass.session_length_minutes * 60_000;
  const bufMs = BOOKING_CFG.bufferMin * 60_000;
  const rangeStart = nowMs + BOOKING_CFG.leadMin * 60_000;
  let rangeEnd = nowMs + daysAhead * 86_400_000;
  if (pass.expires_at) {
    const exp = new Date(pass.expires_at).getTime();
    if (exp < rangeEnd) rangeEnd = exp;
  }

  const slots: Slot[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  for (let d = 0; d <= daysAhead && slots.length < BOOKING_CFG.maxSlots; d++) {
    const dayAnchor = new Date(rangeStart + d * 86_400_000);
    const { y, m, d: dd, dow } = hostLocalParts(hostTz, dayAnchor);
    const dayRanges = rangesByDow[dow] ?? [];
    for (const rng of dayRanges) {
      // Step on the 30-min grid; require the whole session to fit in the range.
      const firstGridMinute = Math.ceil(rng.startMin / BOOKING_CFG.stepMin) * BOOKING_CFG.stepMin;
      for (let mins = firstGridMinute; mins + pass.session_length_minutes <= rng.endMin; mins += BOOKING_CFG.stepMin) {
        const startUtc = hostLocalToUtc(hostTz, y, m, dd, mins).getTime();
        if (startUtc < rangeStart) continue;
        if (startUtc + sessionMs > rangeEnd) break;
        // 15-min buffer around each busy block.
        const conflict = busy.some((b) => b.start - bufMs < startUtc + sessionMs && b.end + bufMs > startUtc);
        if (!conflict) {
          const startIso = new Date(startUtc).toISOString();
          if (!seen.has(startIso)) {
            seen.add(startIso);
            slots.push({ start_at: startIso, end_at: new Date(startUtc + sessionMs).toISOString() });
          }
        }
        if (slots.length >= BOOKING_CFG.maxSlots) break;
      }
      if (slots.length >= BOOKING_CFG.maxSlots) break;
    }
  }
  slots.sort((a, b) => a.start_at.localeCompare(b.start_at));
  return slots;
}

// Parse the get_booking_availability() RPC payload into rangesByDow + tz.
export function parseAvailability(avail: any): { hostTz: string; rangesByDow: Record<number, { startMin: number; endMin: number }[]> } {
  const rangesByDow: Record<number, { startMin: number; endMin: number }[]> = {};
  let hostTz = "America/Toronto";
  if (avail?.timezone) hostTz = avail.timezone;
  for (const r of (avail?.ranges ?? []) as any[]) {
    const [sh, sm] = String(r.start_time).split(":").map(Number);
    const [eh, em] = String(r.end_time).split(":").map(Number);
    (rangesByDow[r.day_of_week] ??= []).push({ startMin: sh * 60 + sm, endMin: eh * 60 + em });
  }
  return { hostTz, rangesByDow };
}

/** True only for a caller holding a valid admin JWT, so an error response can
 *  carry its real cause without leaking it to students. */
async function callerIsAdmin(req: Request): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const authorization = [REDACTED] || "";
    if (!authorization.startsWith("Bearer ")) return false;
    const admin = createClient(
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "",
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? "",
    );
    const caller = await userFromRequest(req);
    if (!caller) return false;
    const { data: role } = await admin
      .from("user_roles")
      .select("role")
      .eq("user_id", caller.id)
      .eq("role", "admin")
      .maybeSingle();
    return !!role;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
  try {
    // Body-size cap (8KB) — reject oversized payloads early. Files never come
    // through here (see booking-upload); this only needs headroom for a book
    // call carrying an optional student_note (validated <=2000 chars below).
    const cl = Number(req.headers.get("content-length") || "0");
    if (cl > 8192) return json({ error: "payload_too_large" }, 413);
    const raw = await req.text();
    if (raw.length > 8192) return json({ error: "payload_too_large" }, 413);
    const body = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};

    const action = String(body.action || "");
    const slug = String(body.slug || "");
    if (!slug || slug.length < 3 || slug.length > 128) return json({ error: "invalid_link" }, 404);

    const supabase = createClient(
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!,
    );

    // Best-available client IP (untrusted; used only for throttling).
    const xff = req.headers.get("x-forwarded-for") || "";
    const ip = xff.split(",")[0].trim() || "unknown";
    const ipHash = await sha256Hex(ip);

    // Invalid-slug lockout check first.
    const invCount = await supabase.rpc("bump_booking_hit", { _ip_hash: ipHash, _slug: "__invalid__", _action: "probe" });
    // (bump on every request; only enforce for misses below.)

    // Resolve identity from the slug (service-only full row).
    const { data: passRow } = await supabase.rpc("resolve_booking_link_full", { _slug: slug });
    // The resolver RETURNS booking_passes, so a non-resolving slug comes back as
    // an all-NULL composite, not an empty result. Over PostgREST that is a
    // truthy object, so `?? null` alone lets a phantom pass through and the
    // redemptions lookup then filters uuid = 'null' and 500s. Require the id.
    const pass: BookingPass | null = passRow?.id ? passRow : null;

    if (!pass) {
      // Count the miss; lock out after too many.
      const misses = await supabase.rpc("bump_booking_hit", { _ip_hash: ipHash, _slug: "__invalid__", _action: "miss" });
      if ((misses.data ?? 0) > LIMITS.invalid) return json({ error: "rate_limited" }, 429);
      return json({ error: "invalid_link" }, 404);
    }

    // Per-(ip,slug,action) rate limit.
    const isBook = action === "book";
    const { data: hitCount } = await supabase.rpc("bump_booking_hit", {
      _ip_hash: ipHash, _slug: slug, _action: isBook ? "book" : "read",
    });
    if (isBook && (hitCount ?? 0) > LIMITS.book) return json({ error: "rate_limited" }, 429);
    if (!isBook && (hitCount ?? 0) > LIMITS.read) return json({ error: "rate_limited" }, 429);

    const creditsRemaining = Math.max(0, pass.total_credits - pass.used_credits);

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------- info

_pass.used_credits; total_credits := _pass.total_credits;\n    status := _pass.status; was_existing := true; RETURN NEXT; RETURN;\n  END IF;\n  IF _pass.status <> 'active' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'pass_not_active:%', _pass.status; END IF;\n  IF _pass.expires_at IS NOT NULL AND _pass.expires_at < now() THEN\n    UPDATE public.booking_passes SET status = 'expired' WHERE id = _pass_id;\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'pass_expired';\n  END IF;\n  IF _pass.used_credits >= _pass.total_credits THEN\n    UPDATE public.booking_passes SET status = 'exhausted' WHERE id = _pass_id;\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'pass_exhausted';\n  END IF;\n  _end_at := _start_at + make_interval(mins => _pass.session_length_minutes);\n  IF NOT public.is_slot_bookable(_start_at, _pass.session_length_minutes) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_unavailable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0425';\n  END IF;\n\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_start_at));\n  IF EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM public.single_session_bookings b\n    WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open', 'booked')\n      AND tstzrange(b.start_at, b.end_at, '[)')\n          && tstzrange(_start_at - interval '15 minutes', _end_at + interval '15 minutes', '[)')\n  ) OR EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions r\n    WHERE r.status = 'booked'\n      AND tstzrange(r.start_at - interval '15 minutes', r.end_at + interval '15 minutes', '[)')\n          && tstzrange(_start_at - interval '15 minutes', _end_at + interval '15 minutes', '[)')\n  ) OR EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM public.schedule_events se\n    WHERE se.deleted_at IS NULL AND se.status <> 'cancelled'\n      AND (_schedule_event_id IS NULL OR se.id <> _schedule_event_id)\n      AND se.start_at < _end_at + interval '15 minutes'\n      AND se.end_at > _start_at - interval '15 minutes'\n  ) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_conflict' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409'; END IF;\n\n  BEGIN\n    INSERT INTO public.booking_pass_redemptions (\n      booking_pass_id, schedule_event_id, google_event_id, start_at, end_at,\n      status, idempotency_key, student_name_snapshot, student_email_snapshot,\n      gcal_state, needs_gcal_sync\n    ) VALUES (\n      _pass_id, _schedule_event_id, NULL, _start_at, _end_at, 'booked',\n      _idempotency_key, _student_name, _student_email, 'pending', true\n    ) RETURNING id INTO _new_id;\n  EXCEPTION WHEN unique_violation THEN\n    SELECT * INTO _existing FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions\n      WHERE booking_pass_id = _pass_id AND idempotency_key = _idempotency_key;\n    redemption_id := _existing.id; start_at := _existing.start_at; end_at := _existing.end_at;\n    used_credits := _pass.used_credits; total_credits := _pass.total_credits;\n    status := _pass.status; was_existing := true; RETURN NEXT; RETURN;\n  END;\n  UPDATE public.booking_passes bp SET\n    used_credits = bp.used_credits + 1,\n    status = CASE WHEN bp.used_credits + 1 >= bp.total_credits THEN 'exhausted' ELSE 'active' END\n  WHERE bp.id = _pass_id RETURNING * INTO _pass;\n  redemption_id := _new_id; start_at := _start_at; end_at := _end_at;\n  used_credits := _pass.used_credits; total_credits := _pass.total_credits;\n  status := _pass.status; was_existing := false; RETURN NEXT;\nEND;\n$function$\n","proname":"redeem_booking_pass_v2"}]}

{"command": "SCR=\"C:/Users/Joshu/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/42293442-2c4d-4ca9-a5bb-5b3746a4b627/scratchpad\"; cat > \"$SCR/check.sql\" <<EOF\nwith s(start_at) as (values $(cat \"$SCR/vals.txt\")),\nx as (\n select start_at, start_at + interval '70 minutes' as end_at from s\n)\nselect to_char(x.start_at at time zone 'America/Toronto','YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI') as slot_et,\n public.is_slot_bookable(x.start_at,70) as bookable,\n exists(select 1 from booking_pass_redemptions r where r.status='booked' and tstzrange(r.start_at - interval '15 minutes', r.end_at + interval '15 minutes','[)') && tstzrange(x.start_at - interval '15 minutes', x.end_at + interval '15 minutes','[)')) as redemption_conflict,\n exists(select 1 from schedule_events se where se.deleted_at is null and se.status<>'cancelled' and se.start_at < x.end_at + interval '15 minutes' and se.end_at > x.start_at - interval '15 minutes') as se_conflict,\n exists(select 1 from single_session_bookings b where b.status in ('draft','checkout_open','booked') and tstzrange(b.start_at,b.end_at,'[)') && tstzrange(x.start_at - interval '15 minutes', x.end_at + interval '15 minutes','[)')) as ss_conflict\nfrom x order by x.start_at;\nEOF\ncat \"$SCR/check.sql\" | head -c 600", "description": "Write the RPC-predicate check SQL for each visible slot"}

 select start_at, start_at + interval '70 minutes' as end_at from s
)
select to_char(x.start_at at time zone 'America/Toronto','YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI') as slot_et,
 public.is_slot_bookable(x.start_at,70) as bookable,
 exists(select 1 from booking_pass_redemptions r where r.status='booked' and tstzrange(r.start_at - interval '15 minutes', r.end_at + interval '15 minutes','[)') && tstzrange(x.start_at - interval '15 minutes', x.end_at + interval '15 minutes','[)')) as redemption_conflict,
 exists(select 1 from schedule_events se where se.deleted_at is null and se.status<>'cancelled' and se.start_at < x.end_at + interval '15 minutes' and se.end_at > x.start_at - interval '15 minutes') as se_conflict,

from x order by x.start_at;

    });
  }

  async function removeFile(index: number) {
    const entry = files[index];
    if (!entry) return;
    if (stagedUploadIds.has(entry.clientUploadId) && upload?.discardUpload) {
      try {
        await upload.discardUpload(entry.clientUploadId, bookingKey);
        setStagedUploadIds((current) => {
          const next = new Set(current);
          next.delete(entry.clientUploadId);
          return next;
        });
      } catch (e) {
        toast.error("Could not remove that file. Try again.");
        return;
      }
    }
    setFiles((current) => current.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  }

  async function commit() {
    if (!slotIso) return;
    setBooking(true);
    try {
      if (upload && files.length > 0) {
        const pending = files.filter((entry) => !stagedUploadIds.has(entry.clientUploadId));
        if (pending.length > 0) {
          const uploaded = await upload.uploadFiles(pending, bookingKey);
          setStagedUploadIds((current) => new Set([...current, ...uploaded.clientUploadIds]));
        }
      }
      const trimmedNote = note.trim().slice(0, MAX_NOTE_CHARS);
      await book(slotIso, bookingKey, trimmedNote || undefined);

      setBookedSlot(slotIso);
      setStep("success");
      // Clear staging so a subsequent booking starts fresh; keep bookedSlot for display.
      setFiles([]);
      setStagedUploadIds(new Set());
      setNote("");
      setBookingKey(crypto.randomUUID());

      // The booking is already committed. Refreshing the parent and resolving
      // the Meet URL are best-effort follow-up work; a refresh failure must
      // never tell the student that a successful booking failed or invite a
      // duplicate retry.
      if (onBooked) {
        try {
          const r = await onBooked(slotIso);
          setBookedMeetUrl(typeof r === "string" ? r : null);
        } catch {
          toast.info("You're booked. Calendar details may take a moment to appear.");
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // Never toast a raw message here. An unrecognised backend code, or the
      // bare supabase-js wrapper, both resolve to the plain fallback rather
      // than leaking an internal string like "redeem_failed" at a student.
      const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "";
      toast.error(bookingErrorMessage(msg));
      if (onCommitError) await onCommitError(); // refresh so a taken slot disappears
    } finally {
      setBooking(false);
    }
  }

  const showCounter = note.length > MAX_NOTE_CHARS * 0.8;

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={handleOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent
        aria-describedby={undefined}
        onEscapeKeyDown={(event) => { if (booking) event.preventDefault(); }}
        onPointerDownOutside={(event) => { if (booking) event.preventDefault(); }}

## user

Base directory for this skill: C:\Users\Joshu\.claude\skills\domain-modeling

# Domain Modeling

Actively build and sharpen the project's domain model as you design. This is the *active* discipline — challenging terms, inventing edge-case scenarios, and writing the glossary and decisions down the moment they crystallise. (Merely *reading* `CONTEXT.md` for vocabulary is not this skill — that's a one-line habit any skill can do. This skill is for when you're changing the model, not just consuming it.)

## File structure

Most repos have a single context:

```
/
├── CONTEXT.md
├── docs/
│   └── adr/
│       ├── 0001-event-sourced-orders.md
│       └── 0002-postgres-for-write-model.md
└── src/
```

If a `CONTEXT-MAP.md` exists at the root, the repo has multiple contexts. The map points to where each one lives:

```
/
├── CONTEXT-MAP.md
├── docs/
│   └── adr/                          ← system-wide decisions
├── src/
│   ├── ordering/
│   │   ├── CONTEXT.md
│   │   └── docs/adr/                 ← context-specific decisions
│   └── billing/
│       ├── CONTEXT.md
│       └── docs/adr/
```

Create files lazily — only when you have something to write. If no `CONTEXT.md` exists, create one when the first term is resolved. If no `docs/adr/` exists, create it when the first ADR is needed.

## During the session

### Challenge against the glossary

When the user uses a term that conflicts with the existing language in `CONTEXT.md`, call it out immediately. "Your glossary defines 'cancellation' as X, but you seem to mean Y — which is it?"

### Sharpen fuzzy language

When the user uses vague or overloaded terms, propose a precise canonical term. "You're saying 'account' — do you mean the Customer or the User? Those are different things."

### Discuss concrete scenarios

When domain relationships are being discussed, stress-test them with specific scenarios. Invent scenarios that probe edge cases and force the user to be precise about the boundaries between concepts.

### Cross-reference with code

When the user states how something works, check whether the code agrees. If you find a contradiction, surface it: "Your code cancels entire Orders, but you just said partial cancellation is possible — which is right?"

### Update CONTEXT.md inline

When a term is resolved, update `CONTEXT.md` right there. Don't batch these up — capture them as they happen. Use the format in [CONTEXT-FORMAT.md](./CONTEXT-FORMAT.md).

`CONTEXT.md` should be totally devoid of implementation details. Do not treat `CONTEXT.md` as a spec, a scratch pad, or a repository for implementation decisions. It is a glossary and nothing else.

### Offer ADRs sparingly

Only offer to create an ADR when all three are true:

1. **Hard to reverse** — the cost of changing your mind later is meaningful
2. **Surprising without context** — a future reader will wonder "why did they do it this way?"
3. **The result of a real trade-off** — there were genuine alternatives and you picked one for specific reasons

If any of the three is missing, skip the ADR. Use the format in [ADR-FORMAT.md](./ADR-FORMAT.md).

## assistant

Here's what I found, then the first question.

## What Sandra is referring to

Not Cal.com. She has an LSAT U booking pass ([send-ledger.md:55](wiki/send-ledger.md:55)): `/book/sandra-wc4kt2xjhcbxc4bqbytas9qmad`, 10 credits, sent 2026-09-09. The message she saw, "That time was just taken. Pick another.", is the app's copy for the `slot_conflict` code ([edgeFunctionError.ts:35](projects/lsatu/src/lib/edgeFunctionError.ts:35)).

**Verified from the live database:**
- Her pass is active, **0 of 10 credits used, zero redemptions**. None of her bookings went through.
- The rate-limit log shows her attempts: 1 on 2026-09-09 00:15 ET, then 4 between 21:54 and 21:55 ET on 2026-09-10, right before she emailed.

## The cause

Three places decide whether a slot is free, and they don't agree on the gap required next to an *existing LSAT U booking*:

| Where | Gap required |
|---|---|
| Slot list the student sees (`generateSlots`, [bookingCore.ts:167](projects/lsatu/supabase/functions/_shared/bookingCore.ts:167)) | 15 min |
| Edge-function guard just before reserving ([bookingCore.ts:235](projects/lsatu/supabase/functions/_shared/bookingCore.ts:235)) | 15 min |
| `redeem_booking_pass_v2` RPC, redemptions clause | **30 min** (buffers both sides: `r.start-15` vs `_start-15`) |
| `no_overlapping_bookings` EXCLUDE constraint | **30 min** (same double buffer) |

So any slot 15–29 minutes before or after a session that was booked through LSAT U is offered, then refused. I tested every one of the 109 slots Sandra can see right now against the RPC's exact predicate: **15 are traps**, e.g. Mon 2026-09-14 15:30 (Charles ends 15:10) and 17:00 (Celia starts 18:30). They're the first slot after and last slot before each of your sessions, which is exactly where a student looking for "the next available time" clicks. And when the booking fails, the UI refreshes the list "so the taken slot disappears", but it doesn't disappear, so she can hit it again. This has been in the schema since 2026-07-19; it isn't a regression from this week.

**Not verified:** which specific slots she clicked. The hit log doesn't store the time, and I have no edge-function log access. If she clicked a non-trap slot, there's a second cause I haven't found. Everything else (Google free/busy, the calendar mirror, single sessions) checks out consistent.

**One latent bug I noticed in passing:** the RPC's `schedule_events` clause doesn't exclude events marked "free" (transparent) while the slot list does. Nothing on your calendar is transparent right now, so it didn't bite Sandra.

---

**Question 1 of ~4:** What is the intended gap between two back-to-back sessions when *both* were booked through LSAT U: 15 minutes or 30?

My recommendation: **15**, the same as everywhere else. You seem to run sessions 20 minutes apart already (Charles 14:00–15:10, then a gap, then Celia 18:30 is the Google mirror's rule of 15 applied by Cal.com and Google bookings), and your CONTEXT.md glossary says nothing about a larger gap for pass bookings. The fix is then a migration: change `booking_buffered_range` in the constraint and the RPC clause to buffer one side only. If you actually want 30 minutes between your own sessions, the fix goes the other way: the slot list and guard tighten to match, and you lose those 15 slots honestly instead of by surprise.

# Booking — Context

The language of paying for and scheduling tutoring time. Covers the student booking surface (`/schedule`, `/booking`), the public booking microsite (`/book/:slug`), and the Single Session page.

## Terms

### Single Session
One tutoring session paid for individually ($45 for the 70-minute session) rather than out of a Package, booked from a public shareable link by anyone — prospect or current student — without logging in. Needs no Booking Pass. Replaces the Cal.com "LSAT Session" (`session-link`) event, which stays live in parallel until Joshua retires it. _Avoid (per the root glossary): one-off, ad hoc session, hourly._

### Package
Five or ten hours of tutoring bought upfront through Stripe, consumed by booking sessions out of a Booking Pass.

### Booking Pass
The prepaid entitlement a student holds when sessions are **owed** to them, carrying the count owed and the count used, reachable at its own private high-entropy booking link. Granted by Joshua or by a matched package payment. A Single Session never creates or consumes one. _Avoid: booking link (that is the pass's URL, not the thing), wallet, credits-as-product._

### Booker
The person on the public page picking a slot. Not necessarily an authenticated user or an existing student; identified by the name and email they enter.

### Slot
A concrete start time offered on the calendar. For the Single Session, slots run at a 70-minute pitch from the start of each day's availability window, within the booking window, respecting minimum notice and calendar conflicts.

### Hold
A short-lived claim on a Slot created when a Booker submits their details and is sent to payment. While live it blocks the Slot for everyone else; it converts to a booking when payment succeeds and evaporates if payment doesn't arrive in time.

### Booking Window
How far into the future slots are offered. The Single Session mirrors Cal.com: 6 business days out, non-rolling.

### Minimum Notice
How close to now a Slot may start. The Single Session requires 24 hours.

// Shared booking core, used by BOTH front doors:
//   * student-booking  (JWT identity)   — logged-in students
//   * public-booking   (slug identity)  — anonymous per-student links
//
// The core NEVER derives identity. Each caller resolves an already-authorized
// { pass, identity } and hands it in. Slot generation and the booking pipeline
// live here once (previously duplicated in student-booking + booking-pass).
//
// Booking uses a RESERVE-FIRST state machine (Codex R3 #4): the DB row is
// created first (gcal_state='pending') via redeem_booking_pass_v2 — which
// derives the end time and enforces the full slot policy in-transaction — and
// the Google event is created AFTER, then persisted back. A crash between the
// two leaves a 'pending' row the reconciler adopts; the booking is honored.
//
// The GoogleCalendar dependency is injected so tests can supply a deterministic
// mock and exercise the crash matrix without touching Google.

import { sendAppEmail } from "./sendAppEmail.ts";


export const BOOKING_CFG = {
  sessionMinDefault: 70,
  bufferMin: 15,
  stepMin: 30,
  leadMin: 1440,
  lookaheadDaysDefault: 21,
  maxSlots: 200,
};

export type BookingPass = {
  id: string;
  status: string;
  total_credits: number;
  used_credits: number;
  session_length_minutes: number;
  expires_at: string | null;
  student_id: string | null;
  student_name: string | null;
  student_email: string | null;
};

export type BookingIdentity = {
  student_id: string | null; // null for name/email-only passes (legacy); slug passes carry it
  email: string | null;
  name: string;
};

export type GoogleCal = {
  insertEvent: (calendarId: string, body: unknown, opts?: any) => Promise<any>;
  patchEvent: (calendarId: string, eventId: string, body: unknown, opts?: any) => Promise<any>;
  getEvent: (calendarId: string, eventId: string) => Promise<any>;
  deleteEvent: (calendarId: string, eventId: string, opts?: any) => Promise<any>;
  freeBusy: (calIds: string[], startIso: string, endIso: string) => Promise<Record<string, { start: string; end: string }[]>>;
  meetUrlFromEvent: (event: any) => string | null;
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- timezone utils
// UTC offset (minutes) of an IANA tz at an instant (DST-aware; positive = east).
function tzOffsetMinutesAt(timeZone: string, at: Date): number {
  const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    timeZone, hourCycle: "h23",
    year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit",
    hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit",
  });
  const map: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const p of dtf.formatToParts(at)) if (p.type !== "literal") map[p.type] = Number(p.value);
  const asUtc = Date.UTC(map.year, map.month - 1, map.day, map.hour, map.minute, map.second);
  return Math.round((asUtc - at.getTime()) / 60000);
}

// UTC instant for a host-local calendar date + wall-clock minutes (settles DST).
function hostLocalToUtc(timeZone: string, y: number, m: number, d: number, minutesOfDay: number): Date {
  const naiveUtc = Date.UTC(y, m, d, Math.floor(minutesOfDay / 60), minutesOfDay % 60);
  let guess = new Date(naiveUtc - tzOffsetMinutesAt(timeZone, new Date(naiveUtc)) * 60000);
  guess = new Date(naiveUtc - tzOffsetMinutesAt(timeZone, guess) * 60000);
  return guess;
}

function hostLocalParts(timeZone: string, at: Date) {
  const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    timeZone, year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit", weekday: "short",
  });
  const map: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const p of dtf.formatToParts(at)) map[p.type] = p.value;
  const wd: Record<string, number> = { Sun: 0, Mon: 1, Tue: 2, Wed: 3, Thu: 4, Fri: 5, Sat: 6 };
  return { y: Number(map.year), m: Number(map.month) - 1, d: Number(map.day), dow: wd[map.weekday] };
}

export type Slot = { start_at: string; end_at: string };

    // Idempotent replay: return the already-booked row.
    const { data: existing } = await supabase.from("booking_pass_redemptions").select("*").eq("id", redemptionId).maybeSingle();
    return { ok: true, redemption: existing, was_existing: true };
  }

  // ---- Booking-time student submission: persist the note + link any files
  // staged (by booking-upload) under this (pass, idempotency_key). Done before
  // gcal so a Google failure never loses the student's note/attachments.
  let uploadedFileNames: string[] = [];
  let submissionStatus: "none" | "linked" | "failed" = "none";
  try {
    if (studentNote) {
      const { error: noteErr } = await supabase.from("booking_pass_redemptions")
        .update({ student_note: studentNote }).eq("id", redemptionId);
      if (noteErr) throw noteErr;
    }
    const { data: linked, error: linkErr } = await supabase.from("external_work_uploads")
      .update({ redemption_id: redemptionId, student_id: identity.student_id ?? undefined })
      .eq("booking_pass_id", pass.id)
      .eq("booking_idempotency_key", idem)
      .is("redemption_id", null)
      .select("file_name");
    if (linkErr) throw linkErr;
    uploadedFileNames = (linked ?? []).map((r: any) => r.file_name).filter(Boolean);
    if (studentNote || uploadedFileNames.length > 0) {
      submissionStatus = "linked";
      const { error: statusErr } = await supabase.from("booking_pass_redemptions")
        .update({ submission_status: "linked", submission_error: null }).eq("id", redemptionId);
      if (statusErr) throw statusErr;
    }
  } catch (subErr) {
    submissionStatus = "failed";
    const message = subErr instanceof Error ? subErr.message : String(subErr);
    console.error("linking booking submission failed", { redemptionId, error: message });
    const { error: repairErr } = await supabase.from("booking_pass_redemptions")
      .update({ submission_status: "failed", submission_error: message }).eq("id", redemptionId);
    if (repairErr) console.error("submission repair state failed", repairErr);
    const { error: alertErr } = await supabase.from("booking_calendar_alerts").insert({
      redemption_id: redemptionId, alert_type: "persistence_failure",
      details: { category: "submission_linkage", error: message },
    });
    if (alertErr && alertErr.code !== "23505") console.error("submission repair alert failed", alertErr);
  }

  // Resolve the target calendar.
  const { data: stateRow, error: stateErr } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state").select("calendar_id").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();
  if (stateErr) {
    return { ok: false, error: "calendar_config_failed", status: 503, details: stateErr.message };
  }
  const calendarId = stateRow?.calendar_id || "primary";

  // Calendar event title: "<first name> session" (e.g. "Ashley session").
  const firstName = String(studentName).trim().split(/\s+/)[0] || String(studentName).trim();
  const eventTitle = `${firstName} session`;

  // Sessions the student has left AFTER this booking — stated plainly, no
  // "X of Y" / "remaining" pressure framing.
  const creditsLeft = Math.max(0, (pass.total_credits ?? 0) - (pass.used_credits + 1));
  const description = [
    "Booked via LSAT U.",
    studentEmail ? `Student: ${studentName} <${studentEmail}>` : `Student: ${studentName}`,
    `${creditsLeft} session${creditsLeft === 1 ? "" : "s"} in your package`,
  ].join("\n");

  // ---- STEP 2: GOOGLE (event + Meet, stamped for the reconciler)
  let gEvent: any;
  const deterministicEventId = deterministicGoogleEventId(redemptionId);
  try {
    gEvent = await gcal.insertEvent(calendarId, {
      id: deterministicEventId,
      summary: eventTitle,
      description,
      start: { dateTime: startAt.toISOString() },
      end: { dateTime: endAt.toISOString() },
      conferenceData: { createRequest: { requestId: idem, conferenceSolutionKey: { type: "hangoutsMeet" } } },
      attendees: studentEmail ? [{ email: studentEmail }] : undefined,
      // The stamp the reconciler searches by to adopt-or-create idempotently.
      extendedProperties: { private: { lsatu_redemption_id: redemptionId, lsatu_idempotency_key: idem, lsatu_pass_id: pass.id } },
    }, { conferenceDataVersion: 1, sendUpdates: studentEmail ? "all" : "none" });
  } catch (gErr) {
    if ((gErr as { status?: number }).status === 409) {
      try {
        gEvent = await gcal.getEvent(calendarId, deterministicEventId);
      } catch (adoptErr) {
        console.error("google event conflict could not be adopted", adoptErr);
      }
    }
    if (gEvent) {
      // Adopted the deterministic event created by an earlier timed-out call.
    } else {
    // Booking is RESERVED and honored. Leave the row 'pending' for the
    // reconciler; do NOT roll back a paid slot. Report success with pending sync.
    console.error("google insert failed; left pending for reconciler", gErr);
    return {
      ok: true,
      was_existing: false,
      redemption: {
        id: redemptionId, start_at: startAt.toISOString(), end_at: endAt.toISOString(),
        status: "booked", gcal_state: "pending", meet_url: null, google_event_id: null,
        submission_status: submissionStatus,
      },
    };
    }
  }

  let meetUrl = gcal.meetUrlFromEvent(gEvent);
  if (!meetUrl) {
    try {
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1200));
      meetUrl = gcal.meetUrlFromEvent(await gcal.getEvent(calendarId, gEvent.id));
    } catch (_) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  // Mirror to schedule_events (best-effort; the redemption is the source of truth).
  const { data: se, error: mirrorErr } = await supabase.from("schedule_events").upsert({
    google_event_id: gEvent.id, calendar_id: calendarId,
    title: eventTitle, description,
    start_at: startAt.toISOString(), end_at: endAt.toISOString(),
    all_day: false, status: gEvent.status ?? "confirmed",
    html_link: gEvent.htmlLink ?? null, meet_url: meetUrl,
    source: "booking_pass", etag: gEvent.etag ?? null,
    last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(), deleted_at: null,
  }, { onConflict: "google_event_id" }).select().maybeSingle();

  if (mirrorErr) {
    console.error("booking calendar mirror persistence failed", mirrorErr);
  }

  // ---- STEP 3: PERSIST (guard so a reconciler that already filled it isn't clobbered)
  const { error: persistErr } = await supabase.from("booking_pass_redemptions")
    .update({
      google_event_id: gEvent.id, meet_url: meetUrl, schedule_event_id: se?.id ?? null,
      gcal_state: "synced", needs_gcal_sync: false, gcal_error: null,
      calendar_action: "none", calendar_status: "synced", calendar_error: null,
      calendar_last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(), calendar_lease_until: null,
    })
    .eq("id", redemptionId)
    .is("google_event_id", null);
  if (persistErr || mirrorErr) {
    const message = persistErr?.message || mirrorErr?.message || "calendar_persistence_failed";
    const { error: pendingErr } = await supabase.from("booking_pass_redemptions").update({
      calendar_action: "create", calendar_status: "pending", calendar_error: message,
      gcal_state: "pending", needs_gcal_sync: true, gcal_error: message,
    }).eq("id", redemptionId);
    if (pendingErr) console.error("booking pending-state persistence failed", pendingErr);
    return {
      ok: true,
      was_existing: false,
      redemption: {
        id: redemptionId, start_at: startAt.toISOString(), end_at: endAt.toISOString(),
        status: "booked", calendar_action: "create", calendar_status: "pending",
        meet_url: meetUrl, google_event_id: gEvent.id, submission_status: submissionStatus,
      },
    };
  }

  // Confirmation emails to BOTH student + admin (best-effort).
  const whenText = startAt.toLocaleString("en-US", {
    weekday: "long", month: "long", day: "numeric", hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit",
    timeZone: "America/Toronto", timeZoneName: "short",
  });
  const sendConfirmation = async (recipientEmail: string, recipientRole: "student" | "admin") => {
    try {
      await sendAppEmail("booking-confirmation", recipientEmail, {
        idempotencyKey: `booking-${redemptionId}-${recipientRole}`.slice(0, 200),
        templateData: {
          recipientRole, studentName, studentEmail, whenText,
          durationMinutes: pass.session_length_minutes, meetUrl,
          // Only the admin copy surfaces the submission.
          studentNote: recipientRole === "admin" ? studentNote : "",
          fileNames: recipientRole === "admin" ? uploadedFileNames : [],
        },
      });
    } catch (mailErr) {
      const message = mailErr instanceof Error ? mailErr.message : String(mailErr);
      console.error(`confirmation email to ${recipientRole} failed`, { redemptionId, error: message });
      const { error: alertErr } = await supabase.from("booking_calendar_alerts").insert({
        redemption_id: redemptionId, alert_type: "persistence_failure",
        details: { category: "confirmation_email", recipient_role: recipientRole, error: message },
      });
      if (alertErr && alertErr.code !== "23505") console.error("confirmation email alert failed", alertErr);
    }
  };
  if (studentEmail) await sendConfirmation(studentEmail, "student");
  if (opts.adminEmail) await sendConfirmation(opts.adminEmail, "admin");

  return {
    ok: true,
    was_existing: false,
    redemption: {
      id: redemptionId, start_at: startAt.toISOString(), end_at: endAt.toISOString(),
      google_event_id: gEvent.id, html_link: gEvent.htmlLink ?? null, meet_url: meetUrl,
      status: "booked", submission_status: submissionStatus,
    },
  };
}

// Pure, framework-free slot-generation + link helpers, shared by the client
// and mirrored by the Deno edge core (supabase/functions/_shared/bookingCore.ts).
// Kept in src/ so Vitest can exercise the DST/buffer/boundary logic directly.

export const BOOKING_CFG = {
  sessionMinDefault: 70,
  bufferMin: 15,
  stepMin: 30,
  leadMin: 1440,
  maxSlots: 200,
};

export function durationLabel(mins: number): string {
  const h = Math.floor(mins / 60), m = mins % 60;
  if (h && m) return `${h} hr ${m} min`;
  if (h) return `${h} hr`;
  return `${m} min`;
}

// UTC offset (minutes) of an IANA tz at an instant (DST-aware; positive = east).
export function tzOffsetMinutesAt(timeZone: string, at: Date): number {
  const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    timeZone, hourCycle: "h23",
    year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit",
    hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit",
  });
  const map: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const p of dtf.formatToParts(at)) if (p.type !== "literal") map[p.type] = Number(p.value);
  const asUtc = Date.UTC(map.year, map.month - 1, map.day, map.hour, map.minute, map.second);
  return Math.round((asUtc - at.getTime()) / 60000);
}

export function hostLocalToUtc(timeZone: string, y: number, m: number, d: number, minutesOfDay: number): Date {
  const naiveUtc = Date.UTC(y, m, d, Math.floor(minutesOfDay / 60), minutesOfDay % 60);
  let guess = new Date(naiveUtc - tzOffsetMinutesAt(timeZone, new Date(naiveUtc)) * 60000);
  guess = new Date(naiveUtc - tzOffsetMinutesAt(timeZone, guess) * 60000);
  return guess;
}

export function hostLocalParts(timeZone: string, at: Date) {
  const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    timeZone, year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit", weekday: "short",
  });
  const map: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const p of dtf.formatToParts(at)) map[p.type] = p.value;
  const wd: Record<string, number> = { Sun: 0, Mon: 1, Tue: 2, Wed: 3, Thu: 4, Fri: 5, Sat: 6 };
  return { y: Number(map.year), m: Number(map.month) - 1, d: Number(map.day), dow: wd[map.weekday] };
}

export type Slot = { start_at: string; end_at: string };
export type DowRanges = Record<number, { startMin: number; endMin: number }[]>;

// Mirror of the edge core's generateSlots: 30-min grid inside seeded windows,
// >= 24h lead, session fits fully, 15-min buffer around busy blocks.
export function generateSlots(opts: {
  sessionMin: number;
  nowMs: number;
  hostTz: string;
  rangesByDow: DowRanges;
  busy: { start: number; end: number }[];
  daysAhead: number;
  expiresAtMs?: number | null;
}): Slot[] {
  const { sessionMin, nowMs, hostTz, rangesByDow, busy, daysAhead, expiresAtMs } = opts;
  const sessionMs = sessionMin * 60_000;
  const bufMs = BOOKING_CFG.bufferMin * 60_000;
  const rangeStart = nowMs + BOOKING_CFG.leadMin * 60_000;
  let rangeEnd = nowMs + daysAhead * 86_400_000;
  if (expiresAtMs && expiresAtMs < rangeEnd) rangeEnd = expiresAtMs;

  const slots: Slot[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  for (let d = 0; d <= daysAhead && slots.length < BOOKING_CFG.maxSlots; d++) {
    const dayAnchor = new Date(rangeStart + d * 86_400_000);
    const { y, m, d: dd, dow } = hostLocalParts(hostTz, dayAnchor);
    const dayRanges = rangesByDow[dow] ?? [];
    for (const rng of dayRanges) {
      const firstGridMinute = Math.ceil(rng.startMin / BOOKING_CFG.stepMin) * BOOKING_CFG.stepMin;
      for (let mins = firstGridMinute; mins + sessionMin <= rng.endMin; mins += BOOKING_CFG.stepMin) {
        const startUtc = hostLocalToUtc(hostTz, y, m, dd, mins).getTime();
        if (startUtc < rangeStart) continue;
        if (startUtc + sessionMs > rangeEnd) break;
        const conflict = busy.some((b) => b.start - bufMs < startUtc + sessionMs && b.end + bufMs > startUtc);
        if (!conflict) {
          const startIso = new Date(startUtc).toISOString();
          if (!seen.has(startIso)) {
            seen.add(startIso);
            slots.push({ start_at: startIso, end_at: new Date(startUtc + sessionMs).toISOString() });
          }
        }
        if (slots.length >= BOOKING_CFG.maxSlots) break;
      }
      if (slots.length >= BOOKING_CFG.maxSlots) break;
    }
  }
  slots.sort((a, b) => a.start_at.localeCompare(b.start_at));
  return slots;
}

// Slug: <firstname>-<128-bit code>. The code's entropy is the Bearer [REDACTED]
const CODE_ALPHABET = "abcdefghjkmnpqrstuvwxyz23456789";
export function genCode(chars = 26, rand: () => number = Math.random): string {
  let out = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < chars; i++) out += CODE_ALPHABET[Math.floor(rand() * CODE_ALPHABET.length)];
  return out;
}
export function sanitizeFirstName(name?: string | null, email?: string | null): string {
  const base = (name?.trim().split(/\s+/)[0] || email?.split("@")[0] || "student").toLowerCase();
  return base.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "").slice(0, 20) || "student";
}

export type BookingEventConfig = {
  slug: string;
  label: string;
  lengthMinutes: number;
  /** Null means slots start every lengthMinutes, which is what Cal.com does. */
  slotIntervalMinutes: number | null;
  minimumNoticeMinutes: number;
  bufferBeforeMinutes: number;
  bufferAfterMinutes: number;
  windowBusinessDays: number;
  priceCents: number;
  currency: string;
  requiresPayment: boolean;
  /** A one-off link: sellable exactly once, then spent. */
  singleUse: boolean;
  /**
   * The one instant this link sells, as an ISO string. When set, the link
   * offers only this time and the notice/window/weekly-hours rules do not
   * apply -- the time was chosen by hand. Null for an ordinary event.
   */
  pinnedStartAt: string | null;
};

export const SINGLE_SESSION_EVENT: BookingEventConfig = {
  slug: "single-session",
  label: "LSAT Session",
  lengthMinutes: SINGLE_SESSION.durationMinutes,
  slotIntervalMinutes: null,
  minimumNoticeMinutes: SINGLE_SESSION.leadMinutes,
  bufferBeforeMinutes: 0,
  bufferAfterMinutes: 0,
  windowBusinessDays: SINGLE_SESSION.businessDays,
  priceCents: SINGLE_SESSION.amountCents,
  currency: SINGLE_SESSION.currency,
  requiresPayment: true,
  singleUse: false,
  pinnedStartAt: null,
};

/** Maps a booking_event_types row onto the shape the slot generator wants. */
export function toEventConfig(row: Record<string, unknown>): BookingEventConfig {
  return {
    slug: String(row.slug),
    label: String(row.label ?? ""),
    lengthMinutes: Number(row.length_minutes),
    slotIntervalMinutes: row.slot_interval_minutes == null ? null : Number(row.slot_interval_minutes),
    minimumNoticeMinutes: Number(row.minimum_notice_minutes),
    bufferBeforeMinutes: Number(row.buffer_before_minutes ?? 0),
    bufferAfterMinutes: Number(row.buffer_after_minutes ?? 0),
    windowBusinessDays: Number(row.window_business_days),
    priceCents: Number(row.price_cents ?? 0),
    currency: String(row.currency ?? "usd"),
    requiresPayment: row.requires_payment !== false,
    singleUse: row.single_use === true,
    pinnedStartAt: row.pinned_start_at ? new Date(String(row.pinned_start_at)).toISOString() : null,
  };
}

const EMAIL = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

export function normalizeEmail(value: unknown): string {
  const email = String(value ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!EMAIL.test(email) || email.length > 254) throw new Error("invalid_email");
  return email;
}

export function normalizeGuests(values: unknown, bookerEmail: string): string[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(values)) return [];
  const booker = normalizeEmail(bookerEmail);
  const unique = new Set<string>();
  for (const value of values) {
    const email = normalizeEmail(value);
    if (email !== booker) unique.add(email);
  }
  if (unique.size > 10) throw new Error("too_many_guests");
  return [...unique];
}

function tzOffsetMinutesAt(timeZone: string, at: Date): number {
  const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    timeZone,
    hourCycle: "h23",
    year: "numeric",
    month: "2-digit",
    day: "2-digit",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
    second: "2-digit",
  });
  const parts: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const part of dtf.formatToParts(at)) {
    if (part.type !== "literal") parts[part.type] = Number(part.value);
  }
  return Math.round((Date.UTC(parts.year, parts.month - 1, parts.day, parts.hour, parts.minute, parts.second) - at.getTime()) / 60_000);
}

function hostLocalToUtc(timeZone: string, year: number, month: number, day: number, minutes: number): Date {
  const wallClockAsUtc = Date.UTC(year, month, day, Math.floor(minutes / 60), minutes % 60);
  let guess = new Date(wallClockAsUtc - tzOffsetMinutesAt(timeZone, new Date(wallClockAsUtc)) * 60_000);
  guess = new Date(wallClockAsUtc - tzOffsetMinutesAt(timeZone, guess) * 60_000);
  return guess;
}

function hostDateParts(timeZone: string, at: Date) {
  const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    timeZone,
    year: "numeric",
    month: "2-digit",
    day: "2-digit",
    weekday: "short",
  });
  const parts: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const part of dtf.formatToParts(at)) parts[part.type] = part.value;
  const weekday: Record<string, number> = { Sun: 0, Mon: 1, Tue: 2, Wed: 3, Thu: 4, Fri: 5, Sat: 6 };
  return { year: Number(parts.year), month: Number(parts.month) - 1, day: Number(parts.day), dow: weekday[parts.weekday] };
}

    }
  }

  return slots.sort((left, right) => left.start_at.localeCompare(right.start_at));
}

function eventBounds(event: any): { start: number; end: number } | null {
  const start = Date.parse(event?.start?.dateTime ?? event?.start?.date ?? "");
  const end = Date.parse(event?.end?.dateTime ?? event?.end?.date ?? "");
  return Number.isFinite(start) && Number.isFinite(end) ? { start, end } : null;
}

export function hasConflictingCalendarEvent(
  events: any[],
  startAt: string,
  endAt: string,
  ownEventId?: string | null,
): boolean {
  const start = Date.parse(startAt);
  const end = Date.parse(endAt);
  return events.some((event) => {
    if (!event || event.status === "cancelled" || event.transparency === "transparent" || event.id === ownEventId) return false;
    const bounds = eventBounds(event);
    return !!bounds && bounds.start < end && bounds.end > start;
  });
}

export function parseStripeSignature(header: string | null): { timestamp: number; signatures: string[] } | null {
  if (!header) return null;
  let timestamp = NaN;
  const signatures: string[] = [];
  for (const part of header.split(",")) {
    const splitAt = part.indexOf("=");
    if (splitAt < 1) continue;
    const key = part.slice(0, splitAt).trim();
    const value = part.slice(splitAt + 1).trim();
    if (key === "t") timestamp = Number(value);
    if (key === "v1" && value) signatures.push(value);
  }
  return Number.isFinite(timestamp) && signatures.length ? { timestamp, signatures } : null;
}

export type CheckoutFingerprintInput = {
  bookingId: string;
  description: string;
  email: string;
  successUrl: string;
  cancelUrl: string;
  expiresAt: number;
};

export function buildCheckoutFingerprint(input: CheckoutFingerprintInput) {
  return {
    mode: "payment" as const,
    payment_method_types: ["card"],
    line_items: [{
      quantity: 1,
      price_data: {
        currency: SINGLE_SESSION.currency,
        unit_amount: SINGLE_SESSION.amountCents,
        product_data: { name: input.description },
      },
    }],
    customer_email: normalizeEmail(input.email),
    metadata: { booking_id: input.bookingId, product: "single_session" },
    payment_intent_data: { metadata: { booking_id: input.bookingId, product: "single_session" } },
    success_url: input.successUrl,
    cancel_url: input.cancelUrl,
    expires_at: input.expiresAt,
  };
}

export function toPublicBooking(row: any) {
  return {
    id: String(row.id),
    status: String(row.status),
    start_at: row.start_at,
    end_at: row.end_at,
    checkout_session_id: row.stripe_checkout_session_id ?? null,
    meet_url: row.status === "booked" ? row.meet_url ?? null : null,
  };
}

export type CheckoutValidation =
  | { ok: true; bookingId: string }
  | { ok: false; error: string };

export function validateSingleSessionCheckout(session: any, expectedLivemode: boolean): CheckoutValidation {
  if (session?.metadata?.product !== "single_session" || !session?.metadata?.booking_id) {
    return { ok: false, error: "invalid_product" };
  }
  if (session.mode !== "payment") return { ok: false, error: "invalid_mode" };
  if (session.payment_status !== "paid") return { ok: false, error: "payment_not_complete" };
  if (session.amount_total !== SINGLE_SESSION.amountCents) return { ok: false, error: "amount_mismatch" };
  if (String(session.currency || "").toLowerCase() !== SINGLE_SESSION.currency) return { ok: false, error: "currency_mismatch" };
  if (Boolean(session.livemode) !== expectedLivemode) return { ok: false, error: "livemode_mismatch" };
  return { ok: true, bookingId: String(session.metadata.booking_id) };
}

export function deterministicSingleSessionEventId(bookingId: string): string {
  return `ss${bookingId.replace(/-/g, "").toLowerCase()}`;
}

    if (!authorized) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: "unauthorized" }), {
        status: 401, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      });
    }


    const { data: stateRow } = await supabase
      .from("gcal_sync_state").select("*").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();
    const calendarId = stateRow?.calendar_id || "primary";
    let syncToken: string | null = stateRow?.sync_token ?? null;
    const fullTimeMin = new Date(Date.now() - 14 * 24 * 3600 * 1000).toISOString();
    const fullTimeMax = new Date(Date.now() + 90 * 24 * 3600 * 1000).toISOString();
    const { data: availabilityConfig } = await supabase.from("booking_availability_config")
      .select("timezone").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();
    const calendarTimeZone = availabilityConfig?.timezone || "America/Toronto";

    let pageToken: string | undefined;
    let nextSyncToken: string | undefined;
    let processed = 0;
    let usedFallback = false;
    const errors: string[] = [];

    const { error: attemptErr } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state")
      .update({ last_attempt_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq("id", 1);
    if (attemptErr) throw attemptErr;

    do {
      const params: Record<string, string> = syncToken
        ? { singleEvents: "true", maxResults: "250", syncToken }
        : { singleEvents: "true", maxResults: "250", timeMin: fullTimeMin, timeMax: fullTimeMax, orderBy: "startTime" };
      if (pageToken) params.pageToken = pageToken;

      let body: any;
      try {
        body = await listEvents(calendarId, params);
      } catch (err) {
        const status = (err as { status?: number }).status;
        if (status === 410 && syncToken) {
          // syncToken expired — clear and retry full
          syncToken = null;
          pageToken = undefined;
          usedFallback = true;
          const { error: clearErr } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state")
            .update({ sync_token: null, sync_token_updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq("id", 1);
          if (clearErr) throw clearErr;
          continue;
        }
        throw err;
      }

      const items = body.items ?? [];
      for (const ev of items) {
        const stamp = ev.extendedProperties?.private?.lsatu_redemption_id as string | undefined;
        if (ev.status === "cancelled") {
          const { error: cancelErr } = await supabase.from("schedule_events")
            .update({ deleted_at: new Date().toISOString(), status: "cancelled" })
            .eq("google_event_id", ev.id);
          if (cancelErr) {
            console.error("gcal-sync-pull cancel update failed", { eventId: ev.id, error: cancelErr.message });
            errors.push(`cancel ${ev.id}: ${cancelErr.message}`);
          }
          if (stamp) {
            const { error: reconcileErr } = await supabase.rpc("reconcile_booking_calendar_event", {
              _redemption_id: stamp, _google_event_id: ev.id,
              _start_at: new Date().toISOString(), _end_at: new Date().toISOString(),
              _deleted: true, _meet_url: null,
            });
            if (reconcileErr) errors.push(`reconcile delete ${ev.id}: ${reconcileErr.message}`);
          }
          processed++;
          continue;
        }
        const isAllDay = !!ev.start?.date;
        const eventTimeZone = ev.start?.timeZone || ev.end?.timeZone || calendarTimeZone;
        const startAt = ev.start?.dateTime ?? (ev.start?.date ? zonedMidnightIso(ev.start.date, eventTimeZone) : null);
        const endAt = ev.end?.dateTime ?? (ev.end?.date ? zonedMidnightIso(ev.end.date, eventTimeZone) : null);
        if (!startAt || !endAt) continue;

        const { error: upsertErr } = await supabase.from("schedule_events").upsert({
          google_event_id: ev.id,
          calendar_id: calendarId,
          title: ev.summary ?? "(no title)",
          description: ev.description ?? null,
          location: ev.location ?? null,
          start_at: startAt,
          end_at: endAt,
          all_day: isAllDay,
          transparency: ev.transparency === "transparent" ? "transparent" : "opaque",
          status: ev.status ?? "confirmed",
          html_link: ev.htmlLink ?? null,
          source: "google",
          etag: ev.etag ?? null,
          last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          deleted_at: null,
          meet_url: meetUrlFromEvent(ev),
        }, { onConflict: "google_event_id" });
        if (upsertErr) {
          console.error("gcal-sync-pull upsert failed", { eventId: ev.id, error: upsertErr.message });
          errors.push(`upsert ${ev.id}: ${upsertErr.message}`);
        }
        if (stamp) {
          const { error: reconcileErr } = await supabase.rpc("reconcile_booking_calendar_event", {
            _redemption_id: stamp, _google_event_id: ev.id,
            _start_at: startAt, _end_at: endAt, _deleted: false,
            _meet_url: meetUrlFromEvent(ev),
          });
          if (reconcileErr) errors.push(`reconcile move ${ev.id}: ${reconcileErr.message}`);
        }
        processed++;
      }

      pageToken = body.nextPageToken;
      if (!pageToken) nextSyncToken = body.nextSyncToken;
    } while (pageToken);

    // Guard the sync token: only advance if every write succeeded, otherwise
    // keep the old token so the next run retries the same window.
    if (errors.length > 0) {
      console.error("gcal-sync-pull completed with write errors, sync_token NOT advanced", { processed, errorCount: errors.length });
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, processed, errors }), {
        status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      });
    }

    const { error: stateErr } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state").update({
      sync_token: nextSyncToken ?? syncToken ?? null,
      sync_token_updated_at: nextSyncToken ? new Date().toISOString() : stateRow?.sync_token_updated_at,
      last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      last_full_sync_at: usedFallback || !syncToken ? new Date().toISOString() : stateRow?.last_full_sync_at,
    }).eq("id", 1);
    if (stateErr) {
      console.error("gcal-sync-pull final state update failed", stateErr);
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, processed, error: `sync_state update failed: ${stateErr.message}` }), {
        status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      });
    }

    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, processed, calendarId }), {
      headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("gcal-sync-pull error", e);
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) }), {
      status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    });
  }
});

IF;\n\n  IF EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM public.single_session_bookings b\n    WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open', 'booked')\n      AND tstzrange(b.start_at, b.end_at, '[)') && tstzrange(_start_at, _end_at, '[)')\n  ) OR EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions r\n    WHERE r.status = 'booked'\n      AND tstzrange(r.start_at - interval '15 minutes', r.end_at + interval '15 minutes', '[)')\n          && tstzrange(_start_at, _end_at, '[)')\n  ) OR EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM public.schedule_events se\n    WHERE se.deleted_at IS NULL AND se.status <> 'cancelled'\n      AND se.start_at < _end_at AND se.end_at > _start_at\n  ) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_conflict' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  INSERT INTO public.single_session_bookings (\n    start_at, end_at, name, email, notes, guests, client_idempotency_key,\n    draft_expires_at, user_id, booking_token_hash, booking_token_expires_at, ip_hash,\n    event_slug\n  ) VALUES (\n    _start_at, _end_at, trim(_name), _email_normalized, NULLIF(trim(_notes), ''),\n    _guests_normalized, _client_idempotency_key, now() + interval '5 minutes',\n    _user_id, _booking_token_hash, now() + interval '24 hours', _ip_hash,\n    _event_slug\n  ) RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n\n  RETURN _booking;\nEXCEPTION WHEN exclusion_violation OR unique_violation THEN\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings\n  WHERE client_idempotency_key = _client_idempotency_key;\n  IF FOUND THEN RETURN _booking; END IF;\n  RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_conflict' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\nEND;\n$function$\n","proname":"claim_single_session_slot"},{"def":"CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(_start_at timestamp with time zone, _now timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(), _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'::text)\n RETURNS boolean\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\n STABLE\n SET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\n    'America/Toronto'\n  );\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n  _length integer;\n  _pitch integer;\n  _local_start timestamp;\n  _local_date date;\n  _local_time time;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug AND active;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  IF _start_at IS NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  -- The pinned instant, and nothing else. Still refuse to sell one in the past.\n  IF _event.pinned_start_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    RETURN _start_at = _event.pinned_start_at AND _start_at > _now;\n  END IF;\n\n  _length := _event.length_minutes;\n  -- No explicit interval means slots start every length_minutes, which is what\n  -- Cal.com does and what produces the session's 1:00 / 2:10 / 3:20 chain.\n  _pitch := COALESCE(_event.slot_interval_minutes, _event.length_minutes);\n\n  IF _start_at < _now + make_interval(mins => _event.minimum_notice_minutes) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  _local_start := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;\n  _local_date := _local_start::date;\n  _local_time := _local_start::time;\n\n  IF _local_date < (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date\n     OR _local_date > public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now, _event_slug) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1\n    FROM public.booking_availability a\n    WHERE a.day_of_week = extract(dow FROM _local_date)::integer\n      AND _local_time >= a.start_time\n      AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _length) <= a.end_time\n      AND mod(\n        floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - a.start_time)) / 60)::integer,\n        _pitch\n      ) = 0\n  );\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  -- Invalid timezone/configuration is not permission to sell a slot.\n  RETURN false;\nEND;\n$function$\n","proname":"is_single_session_slot_bookable"},{"def":"CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.set_booking_availability(_timezone text, _ranges jsonb)\n RETURNS jsonb\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\n SECURITY DEFINER\n SET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE _overlap boolean;\nBEGIN\n  IF _timezone IS NOT NULL AND length(_timezone) > 0 THEN PERFORM now() AT TIME ZONE _timezone; END IF;\n  WITH parsed AS (\n    SELECT (e->>'day_of_week')::int AS dow, (e->>'start_time')::time AS start_time, (e->>'end_time')::time AS end_time\n    FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(_ranges, '[]'::jsonb)) e\n  )\n  SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM parsed a JOIN parsed b\n    ON a.dow = b.dow AND (a.start_time, a.end_time) <> (b.start_time, b.end_time)\n    AND a.start_time < b.end_time AND a.end_time > b.start_time) INTO _overlap;\n  IF _overlap THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'overlapping_availability_ranges' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400'; END IF;\n  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(_ranges, '[]'::jsonb)) e\n    WHERE (e->>'day_of_week')::int NOT BETWEEN 0 AND 6 OR (e->>'end_time')::time <= (e->>'start_time')::time) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_availability_range' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n  IF _timezone IS NOT NULL AND length(_timezone) > 0 THEN\n    UPDATE public.booking_availability_config SET timezone = _timezone, updated_at = now() WHERE id = 1;\n  END IF;\n  DELETE FROM public.booking_availability;\n  INSERT INTO public.booking_availability(day_of_week, start_time, end_time)\n  SELECT (e->>'day_of_week')::int, (e->>'start_time')::time, (e->>'end_time')::time\n  FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(_ranges, '[]'::jsonb)) e;\n  RETURN public.get_booking_availability();\nEND; $function$\n","proname":"set_booking_availability"}]}
