{"rows":[{"def":"CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_free(_booking_id uuid, _expected_version integer, _live_calendar_clear boolean, _cancel_token_hash text, _cancel_payload_ciphertext text)\n RETURNS jsonb\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\n SECURITY DEFINER\n SET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\n  _requires_payment boolean;\n  _can_book boolean := false;\n  _new_version integer;\n  _slot_start timestamptz;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT start_at INTO _slot_start FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'booking_not_found' USING ERRCODE = 'P0404'; END IF;\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_slot_start));\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id FOR UPDATE;\n\n  SELECT requires_payment INTO _requires_payment\n  FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _booking.event_slug;\n  IF _requires_payment IS DISTINCT FROM false THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_required' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n\n  -- Already finished. Say so rather than doing it twice: the browser retries\n  -- this call, and a retry must not enqueue a second confirmation email.\n  IF _booking.status = 'booked' THEN\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n      'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', true);\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _booking.status <> 'draft' OR _booking.lifecycle_version <> _expected_version THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'stale_booking' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NULLIF(_cancel_token_hash, '') IS NULL OR NULLIF(_cancel_payload_ciphertext, '') IS NULL THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'cancel_capability_required' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n\n  -- The same last look the paid path takes: the calendar was clear a moment ago\n  -- and nothing else in this database holds the time.\n  _can_book := _live_calendar_clear\n    AND NOT EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.single_session_bookings b\n      WHERE b.id <> _booking.id AND b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open', 'booked')\n        AND tstzrange(b.start_at, b.end_at, '[)') && tstzrange(_booking.start_at, _booking.end_at, '[)')\n    )\n    AND NOT EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions r\n      WHERE r.status = 'booked'\n        AND tstzrange(r.start_at - interval '15 minutes', r.end_at + interval '15 minutes', '[)')\n            && tstzrange(_booking.start_at, _booking.end_at, '[)')\n    )\n    AND NOT EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.schedule_events se\n      WHERE se.deleted_at IS NULL AND se.status <> 'cancelled'\n        AND (_booking.gcal_event_id IS NULL OR se.google_event_id IS DISTINCT FROM _booking.gcal_event_id)\n        AND se.start_at < _booking.end_at AND se.end_at > _booking.start_at\n    );\n\n  IF NOT _can_book THEN\n    -- Nothing was charged, so there is nothing to refund: the hold simply dies\n    -- and the tentative calendar event is swept.\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\n      status = 'expired', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n      terminal_reason = 'slot_taken_before_confirm'\n    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n    IF _booking.gcal_event_id IS NOT NULL THEN\n      PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_delete', 'system', 'pending',\n        'expired', _booking.lifecycle_version, NULL);\n    END IF;\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n      'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', false);\n  END IF;\n\n  UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\n    status = 'booked', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n    amount_cents = 0, currency = 'usd',\n    cancel_token_hash = _cancel_token_hash, cancel_token_expires_at = b.start_at,\n    terminal_reason = NULL\n  WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n  _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\n\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_promote', 'system', 'pending',\n    'booked', _new_version, NULL);\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_booking_confirmation', 'booker', 'blocked',\n    'booked', _new_version, _cancel_payload_ciphertext);\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_admin_notification', 'admin', 'blocked',\n    'booked', _new_version, NULL);\n\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n    'lifecycle_version', _new_version, 'was_existing', false);\nEND;\n$function$\n","proname":"finalize_single_session_free"},{"def":"CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(), _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'::text)\n RETURNS date\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\n STABLE\n SET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\n    'America/Toronto'\n  );\n  _day date := (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date;\n  _target integer := COALESCE(\n    (SELECT window_business_days FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug), 6\n  );\n  _business_days integer := 0;\nBEGIN\n  WHILE _business_days < _target LOOP\n    IF extract(isodow FROM _day) BETWEEN 1 AND 5 THEN\n      _business_days := _business_days + 1;\n    END IF;\n    IF _business_days < _target THEN _day := _day + 1; END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  RETURN _day;\nEND;\n$function$\n","proname":"single_session_booking_window_end"}]}

{"rows":[{"def":"CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_payment(_booking_id uuid, _provider_event_id text, _stripe_checkout_session_id text, _stripe_payment_intent_id text, _amount_cents integer, _currency text, _livemode boolean, _live_calendar_clear boolean, _cancel_token_hash text, _cancel_payload_ciphertext text)\n RETURNS jsonb\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\n SECURITY DEFINER\n SET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\n  _claimed_booking_id uuid;\n  _can_book boolean := false;\n  _new_version integer;\n  _slot_start timestamptz;\nBEGIN\n  IF NULLIF(_provider_event_id, '') IS NULL\n     OR NULLIF(_stripe_checkout_session_id, '') IS NULL\n     OR NULLIF(_stripe_payment_intent_id, '') IS NULL\n     OR _amount_cents <> 4500 OR lower(COALESCE(_currency, '')) <> 'usd'\n     OR _livemode IS NULL THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_payment_facts' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT start_at INTO _slot_start FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'booking_not_found' USING ERRCODE = 'P0404'; END IF;\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_slot_start));\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id FOR UPDATE;\n\n  SELECT single_session_booking_id INTO _claimed_booking_id\n  FROM public.booking_commerce_events\n  WHERE provider = 'stripe' AND provider_event_id = _provider_event_id\n    AND event_type = 'single_session_paid';\n  IF FOUND AND _claimed_booking_id IS DISTINCT FROM _booking_id THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'provider_event_already_claimed' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _booking.stripe_checkout_session_id IS DISTINCT FROM _stripe_checkout_session_id THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'checkout_mismatch' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _booking.status IN ('booked', 'cancelled', 'refund_pending', 'refunded', 'refund_failed') THEN\n    IF _booking.stripe_payment_intent_id IS DISTINCT FROM _stripe_payment_intent_id\n       OR _booking.amount_cents IS DISTINCT FROM _amount_cents\n       OR lower(_booking.currency) IS DISTINCT FROM lower(_currency)\n       OR _booking.livemode IS DISTINCT FROM _livemode THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_facts_mismatch' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n    END IF;\n    INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (\n      provider, provider_event_id, event_type, offer_id, amount_cents, currency,\n      payload, single_session_booking_id\n    ) VALUES (\n      'stripe', _provider_event_id, 'single_session_paid', 'single-session-native',\n      _amount_cents, lower(_currency),\n      jsonb_build_object('livemode', _livemode, 'checkout_session_id', _stripe_checkout_session_id),\n      _booking_id\n    ) ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n      'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', true);\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _booking.status NOT IN ('checkout_open', 'expired', 'abandoned') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_not_finalizable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (\n    provider, provider_event_id, event_type, offer_id, amount_cents, currency,\n    payload, single_session_booking_id\n  ) VALUES (\n    'stripe', _provider_event_id, 'single_session_paid', 'single-session-native',\n    _amount_cents, lower(_currency),\n    jsonb_build_object('livemode', _livemode, 'checkout_session_id', _stripe_checkout_session_id),\n    _booking_id\n  ) ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\n\n  _can_book := _live_calendar_clear\n    AND (_booking.status = 'checkout_open'\n      OR (_booking.status = 'expired' AND _booking.status_changed_at >= now() - interval '10 minutes'))\n    AND NOT EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.single_session_bookings b\n      WHERE b.id <> _booking.id AND b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open', 'booked')\n        AND tstzrange(b.start_at, b.end_at, '[)') && tstzrange(_booking.start_at, _booking.end_at, '[)')\n    )\n    AND NOT EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions r\n      WHERE r.status = 'booked'\n        AND tstzrange(r.start_at - interval '15 minutes', r.end_at + interval '15 minutes', '[)')\n            && tstzrange(_booking.start_at, _booking.end_at, '[)')\n    )\n    AND NOT EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.schedule_events se\n      WHERE se.deleted_at IS NULL AND se.status <> 'cancelled'\n        AND (_booking.gcal_event_id IS NULL OR se.google_event_id IS DISTINCT FROM _booking.gcal_event_id)\n        AND se.start_at < _booking.end_at AND se.end_at > _booking.start_at\n    );\n\n  IF _can_book THEN\n    IF NULLIF(_cancel_token_hash, '') IS NULL OR NULLIF(_cancel_payload_ciphertext, '') IS NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'cancel_capability_required' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n    END IF;\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\n      status = 'booked', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n      stripe_payment_intent_id = _stripe_payment_intent_id,\n      amount_cents = _amount_cents, currency = lower(_currency), livemode = _livemode,\n      cancel_token_hash = _cancel_token_hash, cancel_token_expires_at = b.start_at,\n      terminal_reason = NULL\n    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n    _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\n\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_promote', 'system', 'pending',\n      'booked', _new_version, NULL);\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_booking_confirmation', 'booker', 'blocked',\n      'booked', _new_version, _cancel_payload_ciphertext);\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_admin_notification', 'admin', 'blocked',\n      'booked', _new_version, NULL);\n  ELSE\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\n      status = 'refund_pending', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n      stripe_payment_intent_id = _stripe_payment_intent_id,\n      amount_cents = _amount_cents, currency = lower(_currency), livemode = _livemode,\n      terminal_reason = 'paid_slot_unavailable'\n    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n    _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\n    UPDATE public.single_session_outbox SET state = 'superseded', updated_at = now()\n      WHERE booking_id = _booking.id AND state IN ('blocked', 'pending', 'failed')\n        AND action IN ('gcal_promote', 'email_booking_confirmation', 'email_admin_notification');\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_delete', 'system', 'pending',\n      'refund_pending', _new_version, NULL);\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'stripe_refund', 'system', 'pending',\n      'refund_pending', _new_version, NULL);\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n    'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', false);\nEND;\n$function$\n"}]}

{"file_path": "C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu-wt-booking\\supabase\\migrations\\20260911000000_booking_policy_mirrors_calcom.sql", "content": "-- Booking policy mirrors Cal.com, and every check reads the same numbers.\n--\n-- Why. On 2026-09-10 a student (Sandra) tried five times to book from her pass\n-- link and was told \"That time was just taken\" each time. Nothing was taken.\n-- The slot list, the edge-function guard and the database RPC each decided\n-- \"is this slot free?\" with their own arithmetic: the list and guard required a\n-- 15-minute gap next to an existing booking, but redeem_booking_pass_v2 and the\n-- no_overlapping_bookings constraint buffered BOTH rows, so a pass booking needed\n-- 30 minutes of clearance from another pass booking. Fifteen of the 109 slots\n-- she could see were offered and then refused.\n--\n-- What changes.\n--   * One Buffer (10 min, Joshua's number, 2026-09-10) in\n--     booking_availability_config, read by every SQL check through\n--     booking_buffer_minutes(). Counted ONCE between any two events.\n--   * One overlap predicate, booking_slot_is_clear(), used by the pass RPC and\n--     all three single-session RPCs instead of four hand-copied clauses. It also\n--     ignores calendar events marked free (transparent), which the slot list\n--     already did and the RPC did not.\n--   * The exclusion constraint buffers each row by half the Buffer, so two pass\n--     bookings need exactly one Buffer between them, not two. GiST needs an\n--     IMMUTABLE expression, so the half-buffer is a literal: change the Buffer\n--     and this constraint must be rebuilt (see the note at the bottom).\n--   * Date overrides (booking_availability_overrides), Cal.com's \"date override\":\n--     rows for a date replace the weekly hours on that date; a row with null\n--     times closes the day. Seeded with the one override Cal.com carries today.\n--   * Slot pitch = session length + Buffer (80 min for the 70-min session),\n--     window 45 calendar days rolling, both mirroring Cal.com's session-link\n--     event. is_slot_bookable() enforces the same grid the list draws.\n--   * booking_event_types gains window_calendar_days; when set it wins over\n--     window_business_days. The single session moves to 45 calendar days,\n--     10/10 buffers and an 80-minute pitch. The consultation keeps its own\n--     Cal.com-mirrored settings (30 min, 30/30, 5 business days).\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- 1. config\nINSERT INTO public.booking_availability_config (id, timezone)\nVALUES (1, 'America/Toronto')\nON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;\n\nALTER TABLE public.booking_availability_config\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS buffer_minutes integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 10,\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS minimum_notice_minutes integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1440,\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS window_days integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 45;\n\nALTER TABLE public.booking_availability_config\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS booking_availability_config_buffer_even;\nALTER TABLE public.booking_availability_config\n  ADD CONSTRAINT booking_availability_config_buffer_even\n  CHECK (buffer_minutes >= 0 AND buffer_minutes % 2 = 0 AND minimum_notice_minutes >= 0 AND window_days BETWEEN 1 AND 365);\n\nUPDATE public.booking_availability_config\nSET buffer_minutes = 10, minimum_notice_minutes = 1440, window_days = 45, updated_at = now()\nWHERE id = 1;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_buffer_minutes()\nRETURNS integer\nLANGUAGE sql STABLE\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $$\n  SELECT COALESCE((SELECT buffer_minutes FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1), 10);\n$$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- 2. overrides\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_availability_overrides (\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n  date date NOT NULL,\n  start_time time,\n  end_time time,\n  note text,\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  CONSTRAINT booking_availability_overrides_times CHECK (\n    (start_time IS NULL AND end_time IS NULL)\n    OR (start_time IS NOT NULL AND end_time IS NOT NULL AND end_time > start_time)\n  )\n);\nCREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS booking_availability_overrides_date_start\n  ON public.booking_availability_overrides (date, COALESCE(start_time, '00:00'::time));\n\nALTER TABLE public.booking_availability_overrides ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"Admins manage availability overrides\" ON public.booking_availability_overrides;\nCREATE POLICY \"Admins manage availability overrides\" ON public.booking_availability_overrides\n  FOR ALL USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'::app_role))\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'::app_role));\n\n-- The override Cal.com carries today (Working Hours schedule 681247).\nINSERT INTO public.booking_availability_overrides (date, start_time, end_time, note)\nVALUES ('2026-09-19', '20:00', '21:10', 'Mirrors the Cal.com date override for Fernande')\nON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;\n\n-- Effective hours for one host-local date: overrides replace the weekly rows.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_ranges_for_date(_date date)\nRETURNS TABLE(start_time time, end_time time)\nLANGUAGE sql STABLE\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $$\n  WITH o AS (\n    SELECT start_time, end_time FROM public.booking_availability_overrides WHERE date = _date\n  )\n  SELECT start_time, end_time FROM o WHERE start_time IS NOT NULL\n  UNION ALL\n  SELECT a.start_time, a.end_time FROM public.booking_availability a\n  WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM o)\n    AND a.day_of_week = extract(dow FROM _date)::integer;\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_booking_availability()\nRETURNS jsonb\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _cfg public.booking_availability_config%ROWTYPE;\n  _result jsonb;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _cfg FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1;\n  _result := jsonb_build_object(\n    'timezone', COALESCE(_cfg.timezone, 'America/Toronto'),\n    'buffer_minutes', COALESCE(_cfg.buffer_minutes, 10),\n    'minimum_notice_minutes', COALESCE(_cfg.minimum_notice_minutes, 1440),\n    'window_days', COALESCE(_cfg.window_days, 45),\n    'ranges', COALESCE((\n      SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(r) ORDER BY r.day_of_week, r.start_time)\n      FROM (\n        SELECT day_of_week,\n               to_char(start_time, 'HH24:MI') AS start_time,\n               to_char(end_time,   'HH24:MI') AS end_time\n        FROM public.booking_availability\n      ) r\n    ), '[]'::jsonb),\n    'overrides', COALESCE((\n      SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(o) ORDER BY o.date, o.start_time)\n      FROM (\n        SELECT to_char(date, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS date,\n               to_char(start_time, 'HH24:MI') AS start_time,\n               to_char(end_time,   'HH24:MI') AS end_time\n        FROM public.booking_availability_overrides\n        WHERE date >= (now() AT TIME ZONE COALESCE(_cfg.timezone, 'America/Toronto'))::date - 1\n      ) o\n    ), '[]'::jsonb)\n  );\n  RETURN _result;\nEND;\n$function$;\n\n-- Admin write path for overrides, shaped like set_booking_availability.\n-- _overrides: [{date, start_time, end_time}] with null times closing the day.\n-- Replaces every override on or after today; past dates are left alone.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.set_booking_availability_overrides(_overrides jsonb)\nRETURNS jsonb\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _tz text := COALESCE((SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1), 'America/Toronto');\n  _today date := (now() AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date;\nBEGIN\n  IF jsonb_typeof(COALESCE(_overrides, '[]'::jsonb)) <> 'array' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_overrides' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n  IF EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(_overrides, '[]'::jsonb)) e\n    WHERE (e->>'date') IS NULL\n       OR ((e->>'start_time') IS NULL) <> ((e->>'end_time') IS NULL)\n       OR ((e->>'start_time') IS NOT NULL AND (e->>'end_time')::time <= (e->>'start_time')::time)\n  ) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_override_range' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n  DELETE FROM public.booking_availability_overrides WHERE date >= _today;\n  INSERT INTO public.booking_availability_overrides (date, start_time, end_time, note)\n  SELECT (e->>'date')::date, (e->>'start_time')::time, (e->>'end_time')::time, e->>'note'\n  FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(_overrides, '[]'::jsonb)) e\n  WHERE (e->>'date')::date >= _today;\n  RETURN public.get_booking_availability();\nEND;\n$function$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- 3. one overlap predicate\n-- True when nothing in this database occupies [_start_at, _end_at) once the\n-- buffers are applied to the probe. Busy rows are NOT buffered themselves, so\n-- the gap between two events is exactly one buffer, never two.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_slot_is_clear(\n  _start_at timestamptz,\n  _end_at timestamptz,\n  _buffer_before_minutes integer,\n  _buffer_after_minutes integer,\n  _ignore_single_session_id uuid DEFAULT NULL,\n  _ignore_google_event_id text DEFAULT NULL,\n  _ignore_schedule_event_id uuid DEFAULT NULL\n)\nRETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $$\n  WITH probe AS (\n    SELECT tstzrange(\n      _start_at - make_interval(mins => GREATEST(_buffer_before_minutes, 0)),\n      _end_at   + make_interval(mins => GREATEST(_buffer_after_minutes, 0)),\n      '[)'\n    ) AS r\n  )\n  SELECT NOT EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM public.single_session_bookings b, probe\n    WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open', 'booked')\n      AND (_ignore_single_session_id IS NULL OR b.id <> _ignore_single_session_id)\n      AND tstzrange(b.start_at, b.end_at, '[)') && probe.r\n  ) AND NOT EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions x, probe\n    WHERE x.status = 'booked'\n      AND tstzrange(x.start_at, x.end_at, '[)') && probe.r\n  ) AND NOT EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM public.schedule_events se, probe\n    WHERE se.deleted_at IS NULL\n      AND se.status <> 'cancelled'\n      AND COALESCE(se.transparency, 'opaque') <> 'transparent'\n      AND (_ignore_google_event_id IS NULL OR se.google_event_id IS DISTINCT FROM _ignore_google_event_id)\n      AND (_ignore_schedule_event_id IS NULL OR se.id <> _ignore_schedule_event_id)\n      AND tstzrange(se.start_at, se.end_at, '[)') && probe.r\n  );\n$$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- 4. exclusion constraint: half a buffer each side\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_buffered_range(_s timestamptz, _e timestamptz)\nRETURNS tstzrange\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE\nSET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'\nAS $$\n  -- Half of booking_buffer_minutes() (10). Literal because GiST demands\n  -- IMMUTABLE; rebuild this constraint if the Buffer ever changes.\n  SELECT tstzrange(_s - interval '5 minutes', _e + interval '5 minutes', '[)');\n$$;\n\nALTER TABLE public.booking_pass_redemptions DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS no_overlapping_bookings;\nALTER TABLE public.booking_pass_redemptions ADD CONSTRAINT no_overlapping_bookings\n  EXCLUDE USING gist (public.booking_buffered_range(start_at, end_at) WITH &&)\n  WHERE (status = 'booked');\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- 5. pass-link slot policy\n-- Mirrors the edge generateSlots exactly: notice, window, effective hours for\n-- the date (overrides first), whole session inside the hours, and the pitch\n-- grid (session length + Buffer) counted from the start of each range.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_slot_bookable(_start_at timestamptz, _len integer)\nRETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _cfg        public.booking_availability_config%ROWTYPE;\n  _tz         text;\n  _buffer     integer;\n  _pitch      integer;\n  _end_at     timestamptz;\n  _local      timestamp;\n  _local_date date;\n  _local_time time;\n  _r          record;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _cfg FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1;\n  _tz     := COALESCE(_cfg.timezone, 'America/Toronto');\n  _buffer := COALESCE(_cfg.buffer_minutes, 10);\n  _pitch  := _len + _buffer;\n\n  IF _start_at IS NULL OR _len IS NULL OR _len <= 0 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF EXTRACT(SECOND FROM _start_at)::int <> 0 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF _start_at < now() + make_interval(mins => COALESCE(_cfg.minimum_notice_minutes, 1440)) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n  IF _start_at > now() + make_interval(days => COALESCE(_cfg.window_days, 45)) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  _end_at     := _start_at + make_interval(mins => _len);\n  _local      := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;\n  _local_date := _local::date;\n  _local_time := _local::time;\n\n  FOR _r IN SELECT * FROM public.booking_ranges_for_date(_local_date) LOOP\n    IF _local_time >= _r.start_time\n       AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _len) <= _r.end_time\n       AND mod(floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - _r.start_time)) / 60)::integer, _pitch) = 0\n    THEN\n      RETURN true;\n    END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  RETURN false;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND;\n$function$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.redeem_booking_pass_v2(\n  _pass_id uuid, _idempotency_key text, _start_at timestamptz,\n  _schedule_event_id uuid, _student_name text, _student_email text\n)\nRETURNS TABLE(redemption_id uuid, start_at timestamptz, end_at timestamptz, used_credits integer, total_credits integer, status text, was_existing boolean)\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _pass public.booking_passes%ROWTYPE;\n  _existing public.booking_pass_redemptions%ROWTYPE;\n  _end_at timestamptz;\n  _new_id uuid;\n  _buffer integer := public.booking_buffer_minutes();\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _existing FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions\n    WHERE booking_pass_id = _pass_id AND idempotency_key = _idempotency_key;\n  IF FOUND THEN\n    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_existing.start_at));\n    SELECT * INTO _pass FROM public.booking_passes WHERE id = _pass_id;\n    redemption_id := _existing.id; start_at := _existing.start_at; end_at := _existing.end_at;\n    used_credits := _pass.used_credits; total_credits := _pass.total_credits;\n    status := _pass.status; was_existing := true; RETURN NEXT; RETURN;\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT * INTO _pass FROM public.booking_passes WHERE id = _pass_id FOR UPDATE;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'pass_not_found'; END IF;\n  SELECT * INTO _existing FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions\n    WHERE booking_pass_id = _pass_id AND idempotency_key = _idempotency_key;\n  IF FOUND THEN\n    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_existing.start_at));\n    redemption_id := _existing.id; start_at := _existing.start_at; end_at := _existing.end_at;\n    used_credits := _pass.used_credits; total_credits := _pass.total_credits;\n    status := _pass.status; was_existing := true; RETURN NEXT; RETURN;\n  END IF;\n  IF _pass.status <> 'active' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'pass_not_active:%', _pass.status; END IF;\n  IF _pass.expires_at IS NOT NULL AND _pass.expires_at < now() THEN\n    UPDATE public.booking_passes SET status = 'expired' WHERE id = _pass_id;\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'pass_expired';\n  END IF;\n  IF _pass.used_credits >= _pass.total_credits THEN\n    UPDATE public.booking_passes SET status = 'exhausted' WHERE id = _pass_id;\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'pass_exhausted';\n  END IF;\n  _end_at := _start_at + make_interval(mins => _pass.session_length_minutes);\n  IF NOT public.is_slot_bookable(_start_at, _pass.session_length_minutes) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_unavailable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0425';\n  END IF;\n\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_start_at));\n  IF NOT public.booking_slot_is_clear(_start_at, _end_at, _buffer, _buffer, NULL, NULL, _schedule_event_id) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_conflict' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  BEGIN\n    INSERT INTO public.booking_pass_redemptions (\n      booking_pass_id, schedule_event_id, google_event_id, start_at, end_at,\n      status, idempotency_key, student_name_snapshot, student_email_snapshot,\n      gcal_state, needs_gcal_sync\n    ) VALUES (\n      _pass_id, _schedule_event_id, NULL, _start_at, _end_at, 'booked',\n      _idempotency_key, _student_name, _student_email, 'pending', true\n    ) RETURNING id INTO _new_id;\n  EXCEPTION WHEN unique_violation THEN\n    SELECT * INTO _existing FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions\n      WHERE booking_pass_id = _pass_id AND idempotency_key = _idempotency_key;\n    redemption_id := _existing.id; start_at := _existing.start_at; end_at := _existing.end_at;\n    used_credits := _pass.used_credits; total_credits := _pass.total_credits;\n    status := _pass.status; was_existing := true; RETURN NEXT; RETURN;\n  END;\n  UPDATE public.booking_passes bp SET\n    used_credits = bp.used_credits + 1,\n    status = CASE WHEN bp.used_credits + 1 >= bp.total_credits THEN 'exhausted' ELSE 'active' END\n  WHERE bp.id = _pass_id RETURNING * INTO _pass;\n  redemption_id := _new_id; start_at := _start_at; end_at := _end_at;\n  used_credits := _pass.used_credits; total_credits := _pass.total_credits;\n  status := _pass.status; was_existing := false; RETURN NEXT;\nEND;\n$function$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- 6. single-session policy\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS window_calendar_days integer;\n\nUPDATE public.booking_event_types\nSET buffer_before_minutes = 10, buffer_after_minutes = 10,\n    slot_interval_minutes = 80, window_calendar_days = 45, updated_at = now()\nWHERE slug = 'single-session';\n\n-- Window end for an event: calendar days when set (Cal.com \"calendarDays\",\n-- rolling), else the business-day count it always had.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now timestamptz DEFAULT now(), _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'::text)\nRETURNS date\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\n    'America/Toronto'\n  );\n  _day date := (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date;\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n  _target integer;\n  _business_days integer := 0;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug;\n  IF FOUND AND _event.window_calendar_days IS NOT NULL THEN\n    RETURN _day + _event.window_calendar_days;\n  END IF;\n  _target := COALESCE(_event.window_business_days, 6);\n  WHILE _business_days < _target LOOP\n    IF extract(isodow FROM _day) BETWEEN 1 AND 5 THEN\n      _business_days := _business_days + 1;\n    END IF;\n    IF _business_days < _target THEN _day := _day + 1; END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  RETURN _day;\nEND;\n$function$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(_start_at timestamptz, _now timestamptz DEFAULT now(), _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'::text)\nRETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\n    'America/Toronto'\n  );\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n  _length integer;\n  _pitch integer;\n  _local_start timestamp;\n  _local_date date;\n  _local_time time;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug AND active;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF _start_at IS NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  IF _event.pinned_start_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    RETURN _start_at = _event.pinned_start_at AND _start_at > _now;\n  END IF;\n\n  _length := _event.length_minutes;\n  _pitch := COALESCE(_event.slot_interval_minutes, _event.length_minutes);\n\n  IF _start_at < _now + make_interval(mins => _event.minimum_notice_minutes) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  _local_start := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;\n  _local_date := _local_start::date;\n  _local_time := _local_start::time;\n\n  IF _local_date < (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date\n     OR _local_date > public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now, _event_slug) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1\n    FROM public.booking_ranges_for_date(_local_date) a\n    WHERE _local_time >= a.start_time\n      AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _length) <= a.end_time\n      AND mod(\n        floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - a.start_time)) / 60)::integer,\n        _pitch\n      ) = 0\n  );\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND;\n$function$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_slot(_start_at timestamptz, _name text, _email text, _notes text, _guests jsonb, _client_idempotency_key text, _booking_token_hash text, _ip_hash text, _user_id uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'::text)\nRETURNS single_session_bookings\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n  _length integer;\n  _end_at timestamptz;\n  _email_normalized text := lower(trim(_email));\n  _guests_normalized jsonb;\n  _expired record;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug;\n  _length := COALESCE(_event.length_minutes, 70);\n  _end_at := _start_at + make_interval(mins => _length);\n\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings\n  WHERE client_idempotency_key = _client_idempotency_key;\n  IF FOUND THEN\n    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_booking.start_at));\n    SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking.id FOR UPDATE;\n    RETURN _booking;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _start_at IS NULL OR NOT public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(_start_at, now(), _event_slug) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_unavailable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0425';\n  END IF;\n  IF NULLIF(trim(_name), '') IS NULL OR length(trim(_name)) > 200 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_name' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n  IF _email_normalized !~ '^[^@[:space:]]+@[^@[:space:]]+[.][^@[:space:]]+$'\n     OR length(_email_normalized) > 320 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_email' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n  IF length(COALESCE(_notes, '')) > 4000 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'notes_too_long' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n  IF length(COALESCE(_client_idempotency_key, '')) NOT BETWEEN 8 AND 200\n     OR length(COALESCE(_booking_token_hash, '')) NOT BETWEEN 16 AND 256\n     OR length(COALESCE(_ip_hash, '')) NOT BETWEEN 16 AND 256 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_capability' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(COALESCE(_guests, '[]'::jsonb)) <> 'array'\n     OR jsonb_array_length(COALESCE(_guests, '[]'::jsonb)) > 10 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_guests' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(g.email) ORDER BY g.email), '[]'::jsonb)\n  INTO _guests_normalized\n  FROM (\n    SELECT DISTINCT lower(trim(value #>> '{}')) AS email\n    FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(_guests, '[]'::jsonb))\n    WHERE jsonb_typeof(value) = 'string'\n      AND lower(trim(value #>> '{}')) <> _email_normalized\n      AND lower(trim(value #>> '{}')) ~ '^[^@[:space:]]+@[^@[:space:]]+[.][^@[:space:]]+$'\n  ) g;\n  IF jsonb_array_length(_guests_normalized) <> jsonb_array_length(COALESCE(_guests, '[]'::jsonb)) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_or_duplicate_guests' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_start_at));\n\n  FOR _expired IN\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b\n      SET status = 'expired', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n          terminal_reason = 'authoritative_expiry'\n    WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open')\n      AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) <= now()\n      AND tstzrange(b.start_at, b.end_at, '[)') && tstzrange(_start_at, _end_at, '[)')\n    RETURNING b.id, b.lifecycle_version, b.gcal_event_id\n  LOOP\n    IF _expired.gcal_event_id IS NOT NULL THEN\n      PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(\n        _expired.id, 'gcal_delete', 'system', 'pending', 'expired',\n        _expired.lifecycle_version, NULL\n      );\n    END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n\n  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings b\n      WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open')\n        AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) > now()\n        AND b.ip_hash = _ip_hash) >= 3 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hold_cap_ip' USING ERRCODE = 'P0429';\n  END IF;\n  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings b\n      WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open')\n        AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) > now()\n        AND lower(b.email) = _email_normalized) >= 3 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hold_cap_email' USING ERRCODE = 'P0429';\n  END IF;\n  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings b\n      WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open')\n        AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) > now()) >= 250 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hold_cap_global' USING ERRCODE = 'P0429';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NOT public.booking_slot_is_clear(\n    _start_at, _end_at,\n    COALESCE(_event.buffer_before_minutes, 0), COALESCE(_event.buffer_after_minutes, 0)\n  ) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_conflict' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  INSERT INTO public.single_session_bookings (\n    start_at, end_at, name, email, notes, guests, client_idempotency_key,\n    draft_expires_at, user_id, booking_token_hash, booking_token_expires_at, ip_hash,\n    event_slug\n  ) VALUES (\n    _start_at, _end_at, trim(_name), _email_normalized, NULLIF(trim(_notes), ''),\n    _guests_normalized, _client_idempotency_key, now() + interval '5 minutes',\n    _user_id, _booking_token_hash, now() + interval '24 hours', _ip_hash,\n    _event_slug\n  ) RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n\n  RETURN _booking;\nEXCEPTION WHEN exclusion_violation OR unique_violation THEN\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings\n  WHERE client_idempotency_key = _client_idempotency_key;\n  IF FOUND THEN RETURN _booking; END IF;\n  RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_conflict' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\nEND;\n$function$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_free(_booking_id uuid, _expected_version integer, _live_calendar_clear boolean, _cancel_token_hash text, _cancel_payload_ciphertext text)\nRETURNS jsonb\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n  _can_book boolean := false;\n  _new_version integer;\n  _slot_start timestamptz;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT start_at INTO _slot_start FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'booking_not_found' USING ERRCODE = 'P0404'; END IF;\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_slot_start));\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id FOR UPDATE;\n\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _booking.event_slug;\n  IF _event.requires_payment IS DISTINCT FROM false THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_required' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _booking.status = 'booked' THEN\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n      'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', true);\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _booking.status <> 'draft' OR _booking.lifecycle_version <> _expected_version THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'stale_booking' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NULLIF(_cancel_token_hash, '') IS NULL OR NULLIF(_cancel_payload_ciphertext, '') IS NULL THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'cancel_capability_required' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n\n  _can_book := _live_calendar_clear AND public.booking_slot_is_clear(\n    _booking.start_at, _booking.end_at,\n    COALESCE(_event.buffer_before_minutes, 0), COALESCE(_event.buffer_after_minutes, 0),\n    _booking.id, _booking.gcal_event_id, NULL\n  );\n\n  IF NOT _can_book THEN\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\n      status = 'expired', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n      terminal_reason = 'slot_taken_before_confirm'\n    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n    IF _booking.gcal_event_id IS NOT NULL THEN\n      PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_delete', 'system', 'pending',\n        'expired', _booking.lifecycle_version, NULL);\n    END IF;\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n      'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', false);\n  END IF;\n\n  UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\n    status = 'booked', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n    amount_cents = 0, currency = 'usd',\n    cancel_token_hash = _cancel_token_hash, cancel_token_expires_at = b.start_at,\n    terminal_reason = NULL\n  WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n  _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\n\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_promote', 'system', 'pending',\n    'booked', _new_version, NULL);\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_booking_confirmation', 'booker', 'blocked',\n    'booked', _new_version, _cancel_payload_ciphertext);\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_admin_notification', 'admin', 'blocked',\n    'booked', _new_version, NULL);\n\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n    'lifecycle_version', _new_version, 'was_existing', false);\nEND;\n$function$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_payment(_booking_id uuid, _provider_event_id text, _stripe_checkout_session_id text, _stripe_payment_intent_id text, _amount_cents integer, _currency text, _livemode boolean, _live_calendar_clear boolean, _cancel_token_hash text, _cancel_payload_ciphertext text)\nRETURNS jsonb\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n  _claimed_booking_id uuid;\n  _can_book boolean := false;\n  _new_version integer;\n  _slot_start timestamptz;\nBEGIN\n  IF NULLIF(_provider_event_id, '') IS NULL\n     OR NULLIF(_stripe_checkout_session_id, '') IS NULL\n     OR NULLIF(_stripe_payment_intent_id, '') IS NULL\n     OR _amount_cents <> 4500 OR lower(COALESCE(_currency, '')) <> 'usd'\n     OR _livemode IS NULL THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_payment_facts' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT start_at INTO _slot_start FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'booking_not_found' USING ERRCODE = 'P0404'; END IF;\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_slot_start));\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id FOR UPDATE;\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _booking.event_slug;\n\n  SELECT single_session_booking_id INTO _claimed_booking_id\n  FROM public.booking_commerce_events\n  WHERE provider = 'stripe' AND provider_event_id = _provider_event_id\n    AND event_type = 'single_session_paid';\n  IF FOUND AND _claimed_booking_id IS DISTINCT FROM _booking_id THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'provider_event_already_claimed' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _booking.stripe_checkout_session_id IS DISTINCT FROM _stripe_checkout_session_id THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'checkout_mismatch' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _booking.status IN ('booked', 'cancelled', 'refund_pending', 'refunded', 'refund_failed') THEN\n    IF _booking.stripe_payment_intent_id IS DISTINCT FROM _stripe_payment_intent_id\n       OR _booking.amount_cents IS DISTINCT FROM _amount_cents\n       OR lower(_booking.currency) IS DISTINCT FROM lower(_currency)\n       OR _booking.livemode IS DISTINCT FROM _livemode THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_facts_mismatch' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n    END IF;\n    INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (\n      provider, provider_event_id, event_type, offer_id, amount_cents, currency,\n      payload, single_session_booking_id\n    ) VALUES (\n      'stripe', _provider_event_id, 'single_session_paid', 'single-session-native',\n      _amount_cents, lower(_currency),\n      jsonb_build_object('livemode', _livemode, 'checkout_session_id', _stripe_checkout_session_id),\n      _booking_id\n    ) ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n      'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', true);\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _booking.status NOT IN ('checkout_open', 'expired', 'abandoned') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_not_finalizable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (\n    provider, provider_event_id, event_type, offer_id, amount_cents, currency,\n    payload, single_session_booking_id\n  ) VALUES (\n    'stripe', _provider_event_id, 'single_session_paid', 'single-session-native',\n    _amount_cents, lower(_currency),\n    jsonb_build_object('livemode', _livemode, 'checkout_session_id', _stripe_checkout_session_id),\n    _booking_id\n  ) ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\n\n  _can_book := _live_calendar_clear\n    AND (_booking.status = 'checkout_open'\n      OR (_booking.status = 'expired' AND _booking.status_changed_at >= now() - interval '10 minutes'))\n    AND public.booking_slot_is_clear(\n      _booking.start_at, _booking.end_at,\n      COALESCE(_event.buffer_before_minutes, 0), COALESCE(_event.buffer_after_minutes, 0),\n      _booking.id, _booking.gcal_event_id, NULL\n    );\n\n  IF _can_book THEN\n    IF NULLIF(_cancel_token_hash, '') IS NULL OR NULLIF(_cancel_payload_ciphertext, '') IS NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'cancel_capability_required' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n    END IF;\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\n      status = 'booked', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n      stripe_payment_intent_id = _stripe_payment_intent_id,\n      amount_cents = _amount_cents, currency = lower(_currency), livemode = _livemode,\n      cancel_token_hash = _cancel_token_hash, cancel_token_expires_at = b.start_at,\n      terminal_reason = NULL\n    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n    _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\n\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_promote', 'system', 'pending',\n      'booked', _new_version, NULL);\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_booking_confirmation', 'booker', 'blocked',\n      'booked', _new_version, _cancel_payload_ciphertext);\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_admin_notification', 'admin', 'blocked',\n      'booked', _new_version, NULL);\n  ELSE\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\n      status = 'refund_pending', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n      stripe_payment_intent_id = _stripe_payment_intent_id,\n      amount_cents = _amount_cents, currency = lower(_currency), livemode = _livemode,\n      terminal_reason = 'paid_slot_unavailable'\n    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n    _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\n    UPDATE public.single_session_outbox SET state = 'superseded', updated_at = now()\n      WHERE booking_id = _booking.id AND state IN ('blocked', 'pending', 'failed')\n        AND action IN ('gcal_promote', 'email_booking_confirmation', 'email_admin_notification');\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_delete', 'system', 'pending',\n      'refund_pending', _new_version, NULL);\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'stripe_refund', 'system', 'pending',\n      'refund_pending', _new_version, NULL);\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n    'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', false);\nEND;\n$function$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- 7. the phantom rows that blocked Saturdays\n-- Two \"Andrew\" occurrences mirrored on 2026-06-22 from a recurring Google event\n-- that has since been removed from the calendar. The pull sync never tombstoned\n-- them (fixed in gcal-sync-pull alongside this migration); they hid every\n-- Saturday slot on 09-12 and 09-19 from students while Cal.com offered them.\nUPDATE public.schedule_events\nSET deleted_at = now(), status = 'cancelled'\nWHERE deleted_at IS NULL\n  AND source = 'google'\n  AND google_event_id IN ('4gehvktovf170bsvkju1d2utuq_20260912T184500Z', '4gehvktovf170bsvkju1d2utuq_20260919T184500Z');\n\n-- NOTE on changing the Buffer later: update booking_availability_config.buffer_minutes,\n-- then re-create booking_buffered_range with half the new value and rebuild\n-- no_overlapping_bookings (steps 4 above). Everything else reads the config row.\n"}

Every front door\n// (pass link, in-app schedule, single session, consultation) builds one of\n// these and runs the same generateSlots, and the SQL side (is_slot_bookable /\n// is_single_session_slot_bookable + booking_slot_is_clear) enforces the same\n// arithmetic, so nothing a student can click is later refused as a conflict.\nexport type SlotPolicy = {\n  lengthMin: number;\n  /** Grid step from the start of each availability range. */\n  pitchMin: number;\n  /** Clearance required between the session and anything before / after it. */\n  bufferBeforeMin: number;\n  bufferAfterMin: number;\n  /** Minimum notice: no slot starts before now + leadMin. */\n  leadMin: number;\n  /** Last instant a session may end (rolling window, pass expiry). */\n  windowEndMs: number;\n  maxSlots?: number;\n};\n\nexport type DowRanges = Record<number, { startMin: number; endMin: number }[]>;\nexport type DateRanges = Record<string, { startMin: number; endMin: number }[]>;\n\n// Weekly hours plus Cal.com-style date overrides. A date present in\n// overridesByDate replaces that day's weekly hours entirely; an empty array\n// closes the day.\nexport type BookingHours = {\n  hostTz: string;\n  rangesByDow: DowRanges;\n  overridesByDate: DateRanges;\n};\n\nexport type BookingAvailability = BookingHours & {\n  bufferMin: number;\n  leadMin: number;\n  windowDays: number;\n};\n\nfunction isoDate(y: number, m: number, d: number): string {\n  return `${y}-${String(m + 1).padStart(2, \"0\")}-${String(d).padStart(2, \"0\")}`;\n}\n\nexport function rangesForDate(hours: BookingHours, date: string, dow: number) {\n  const override = hours.overridesByDate[date];\n  if (override) return override;\n  return hours.rangesByDow[dow] ?? [];\n}\n\n// Generate bookable slots. Mirrors is_slot_bookable / is_single_session_slot_bookable\n// and booking_slot_is_clear exactly: slots step pitchMin from the start of each\n// range on the host's local date (overrides first), the whole session fits inside\n// the range, it starts no earlier than the notice and ends inside the window,\n// and nothing busy sits within the buffer on either side. Busy blocks are not\n// buffered themselves, so the gap between two events is one buffer, never two.\nexport function generateSlots(opts: {\n  policy: SlotPolicy;\n  hours: BookingHours;\n  nowMs: number;\n  busy: { start: number; end: number }[];\n}): Slot[] {\n  const { policy, hours, nowMs, busy } = opts;\n  const sessionMs = policy.lengthMin * 60_000;\n  const beforeMs = policy.bufferBeforeMin * 60_000;\n  const afterMs = policy.bufferAfterMin * 60_000;\n  const maxSlots = policy.maxSlots ?? BOOKING_CFG.maxSlots;\n  const rangeStart = nowMs + policy.leadMin * 60_000;\n  const rangeEnd = policy.windowEndMs;\n  if (rangeEnd <= rangeStart) return [];\n\n  const slots: Slot[] = [];\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\n  const daysAhead = Math.ceil((rangeEnd - rangeStart) / 86_400_000) + 1;\n  for (let d = 0; d <= daysAhead && slots.length < maxSlots; d++) {\n    // Anchor on the previous day's noon so a DST change never skips a date.\n    const dayAnchor = new Date(rangeStart + d * 86_400_000);\n    const { y, m, d: dd, dow } = hostLocalParts(hours.hostTz, dayAnchor);\n    const dayRanges = rangesForDate(hours, isoDate(y, m, dd), dow);\n    for (const rng of dayRanges) {\n      for (let mins = rng.startMin; mins + policy.lengthMin <= rng.endMin; mins += policy.pitchMin) {\n        const startUtc = hostLocalToUtc(hours.hostTz, y, m, dd, mins).getTime();\n        if (startUtc < rangeStart) continue;\n        if (startUtc + sessionMs > rangeEnd) break;\n        const conflict = busy.some((b) => b.start < startUtc + sessionMs + afterMs && b.end > startUtc - beforeMs);\n        if (!conflict) {\n          const startIso = new Date(startUtc).toISOString();\n          if (!seen.has(startIso)) {\n            seen.add(startIso);\n            slots.push({ start_at: startIso, end_at: new Date(startUtc + sessionMs).toISOString() });\n          }\n        }\n        if (slots.length >= maxSlots) break;\n      }\n      if (slots.length >= maxSlots) break;\n    }\n  }\n  slots.sort((a, b) => a.start_at.localeCompare(b.start_at));\n  return slots;\n}\n\n// The pass link's policy: the pass sets the length, the availability config\n// sets everything else. Pitch is length + buffer so back-to-back slots are all\n// bookable (80 minutes for the 70-minute session).\nexport function passSlotPolicy(pass: BookingPass, avail: BookingAvailability, nowMs: number, daysAhead?: number): SlotPolicy {\n  const days = Math.min(Math.max(daysAhead ?? avail.windowDays, 1), avail.windowDays);\n  let windowEndMs = nowMs + days * 86_400_000;\n  if (pass.expires_at) {\n    const exp = new Date(pass.expires_at).getTime();\n    if (Number.isFinite(exp) && exp < windowEndMs) windowEndMs = exp;\n  }\n  return {\n    lengthMin: pass.session_length_minutes,\n    pitchMin: pass.session_length_minutes + avail.bufferMin,\n    bufferBeforeMin: avail.bufferMin,\n    bufferAfterMin: avail.bufferMin,\n    leadMin: avail.leadMin,\n    windowEndMs,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction toMinutes(hhmm: unknown): number {\n  const [h, m] = String(hhmm).split(\":\").map(Number);\n  return h * 60 + (m || 0);\n}\n\n// Parse the get_booking_availability() RPC payload.\nexport function parseAvailability(avail: any): BookingAvailability {\n  const rangesByDow: DowRanges = {};\n  const overridesByDate: DateRanges = {};\n  const hostTz = avail?.timezone || \"America/Toronto\";\n  for (const r of (avail?.ranges ?? []) as any[]) {\n    (rangesByDow[r.day_of_week] ??= []).push({ startMin: toMinutes(r.start_time), endMin: toMinutes(r.end_time) });\n  }\n  for (const o of (avail?.overrides ?? []) as any[]) {\n    const date = String(o.date);\n    overridesByDate[date] ??= [];\n    if (o.start_time && o.end_time) overridesByDate[date].push({ startMin: toMinutes(o.start_time), endMin: toMinutes(o.end_time) });\n  }\n  return {\n    hostTz,\n    rangesByDow,\n    overridesByDate,\n    bufferMin: Number.isFinite(Number(avail?.buffer_minutes)) ? Number(avail.buffer_minutes) : BOOKING_CFG.bufferMinDefault,\n    leadMin: Number.isFinite(Number(avail?.minimum_notice_minutes)) ? Number(avail.minimum_notice_minutes) : BOOKING_CFG.leadMin,\n    windowDays: Number.isFinite(Number(avail?.window_days)) ? Number(avail.window_days) : BOOKING_CFG.lookaheadDaysDefault,\n  };\n}\n\nexport async function loadAvailability(supabase: any): Promise<BookingAvailability> {\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc(\"get_booking_availability\");\n  if (error) throw error;\n  return parseAvailability(data);\n}\n\n"}

Calendar days when the event says so (Cal.com \"calendarDays\",\n// rolling), else the business-day count, counting today when it is a weekday.\nexport function singleSessionWindowEndDate(nowMs: number, hostTz: string, event: BookingEventConfig, businessDays?: number) {\n  const today = hostDateParts(hostTz, new Date(nowMs));\n  if (event.windowCalendarDays != null) {\n    const d = new Date(Date.UTC(today.year, today.month, today.day + event.windowCalendarDays));\n    return { year: d.getUTCFullYear(), month: d.getUTCMonth(), day: d.getUTCDate() };\n  }\n  const target = businessDays ?? event.windowBusinessDays;\n  let seen = 0;\n  let offset = 0;\n  for (;;) {\n    const d = new Date(Date.UTC(today.year, today.month, today.day + offset));\n    const dow = d.getUTCDay();\n    if (dow >= 1 && dow <= 5) seen++;\n    if (seen >= target) return { year: d.getUTCFullYear(), month: d.getUTCMonth(), day: d.getUTCDate() };\n    offset++;\n  }\n}\n\nexport function generateSingleSessionSlots(opts: {\n  nowMs: number;\n  hostTz: string;\n  rangesByDow: Record<number, { startMin: number; endMin: number }[]>;\n  overridesByDate?: Record<string, { startMin: number; endMin: number }[]>;\n  busy: { start: number; end: number }[];\n  businessDays?: number;\n  /** Defaults to the single session, so existing callers are unaffected. */\n  event?: BookingEventConfig;\n}): SingleSessionSlot[] {\n  const event = opts.event ?? The\n  // weekly hours, the rolling window and the notice all exist to decide whether\n  // a time is one Joshua would agree to; pinning IS that decision, already made.\n  // A clash with something already on the calendar still removes it -- that is\n  // not a policy, it is a fact about the hour.\n  if (event.pinnedStartAt) {\n    const start = Date.parse(event.pinnedStartAt);\n    if (!Number.isFinite(start) || start <= opts.nowMs) return [];\n    const end = start + length * 60_000;\n    const beforePinned = event.bufferBeforeMinutes * 60_000;\n    const afterPinned = event.bufferAfterMinutes * 60_000;\n    if (opts.busy.some((busy) => busy.start < end + afterPinned && busy.end > start - beforePinned)) return [];\n    return [{ start_at: new Date(start).toISOString(), end_at: new Date(end).toISOString() }];\n  }\n\n  // The window reaches to the END of its last date, matching the SQL check\n  // `_local_date > window_end`.\n  const last = singleSessionWindowEndDate(opts.nowMs, opts.hostTz, event, opts.businessDays);\n  const windowEndMs = hostLocalToUtc(opts.hostTz, last.year, last.month, last.day, 24 * 60).getTime();\n  const policy: SlotPolicy = {\n    lengthMin: length,\n    // No explicit interval means slots start every length_minutes, which is\n    // what Cal.com does and what produces the consultation's 1:30 / 2:00 chain.\n    pitchMin: event.slotIntervalMinutes ?? describe("generateSlots", () => {
  const base = { sessionMin: 70, nowMs: NOW, hostTz: EASTERN, rangesByDow: RANGES, busy: [], daysAhead: 21 };

  it("only emits slots on :00 / :30 boundaries in host tz", () => {
    const slots = generateSlots(base);
    expect(slots.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    for (const s of slots) {
      // reconstruct minute-of-hour in host tz
      const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { timeZone: EASTERN, minute: "2-digit", hour12: false }).formatToParts(new Date(s.start_at));
      const min = Number(parts.find((p) => p.type === "minute")?.value);
      expect([0, 30]).toContain(min);
    }
  });

  it("aligns a 13:15 availability start to the 13:30 grid", () => {
    const slots = generateSlots({
      ...base,
      rangesByDow: { 1: [{ startMin: 13 * 60 + 15, endMin: 18 * 60 }] },
    });
    expect(slots.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    for (const slot of slots) {
      const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { timeZone: EASTERN, minute: "2-digit", hour12: false })
        .formatToParts(new Date(slot.start_at));
      expect([0, 30]).toContain(Number(parts.find((part) => part.type === "minute")?.value));
    }
  });

  it("deduplicates slots from overlapping ranges defensively", () => {
    const slots = generateSlots({
      ...base,
      rangesByDow: { 1: [{ startMin: 13 * 60, endMin: 18 * 60 }, { startMin: 14 * 60, endMin: 19 * 60 }] },
    });
    expect(new Set(slots.map((slot) => slot.start_at)).size).toBe(slots.length);
  });

  it("never emits a slot less than 24h from now", () => {
    const slots = generateSlots(base);
    const minStart = Math.min(...slots.map((s) => new Date(s.start_at).getTime()));
    expect(minStart).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(NOW + BOOKING_CFG.leadMin * 60_000);
  });

  it("keeps the full 70-min session inside the availability window", () => {
    const slots = generateSlots(base);
    // Every slot's end must be <= the day's window end; the last Sat slot must
    // start no later than 15:50 (so 70 min ends by 17:00).
    const satSlots = slots.filter((s) => {
      const p = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { timeZone: EASTERN, weekday: "short" }).formatToParts(new Date(s.start_at));
      return p.find((x) => x.type === "weekday")?.value === "Sat";
    });
    expect(satSlots.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    for (const s of satSlots) {
      const startParts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { timeZone: EASTERN, hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", hour12: false }).formatToParts(new Date(s.start_at));
      const h = Number(startParts.find((x) => x.type === "hour")?.value);
      const m = Number(startParts.find((x) => x.type === "minute")?.value);
      expect(h * 60 + m).toBeLessThanOrEqual(15 * 60 + 50); // last valid start for 70min ending 17:00
    }
  });

  it("excludes slots that collide with a busy block (plus 15-min buffer)", () => {
    // Put a busy block on the first Monday 3:00-4:00pm Eastern.
    // Find that Monday relative to NOW.
    const busyStart = hostLocalToUtc(EASTERN, 2026, 6, 6, 15 * 60).getTime(); // Mon Jul 6 2026 15:00 ET
    const busyEnd = busyStart + 60 * 60_000;
    const withBusy = generateSlots({ ...base, busy: [{ start: busyStart, end: busyEnd }] });
    // No slot may overlap [busyStart-15m, busyEnd+15m] with its 70-min span.
    const bufMs = BOOKING_CFG.bufferMin * 60_000;
    for (const s of withBusy) {
      const st = new Date(s.start_at).getTime();
      const en = new Date(s.end_at).getTime();
      const overlaps = busyStart - bufMs < en && busyEnd + bufMs > st;
      expect(overlaps).toBe(false);
    }
  });

  it("respects pass expiry as a hard ceiling", () => {
    const expiresAtMs = NOW + 3 * 86_400_000; // 3 days out
    const slots = generateSlots({ ...base, expiresAtMs });
    const maxEnd = Math.max(...slots.map((s) => new Date(s.end_at).getTime()));
    expect(maxEnd).toBeLessThanOrEqual(expiresAtMs);
  });

  it("produces no slots on Sunday (no seeded window)", () => {
    const sunSlots = generateSlots(base).filter((s) => {
      const p = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { timeZone: EASTERN, weekday: "short" }).formatToParts(new Date(s.start_at));
      return p.find((x) => x.type === "weekday")?.value === "Sun";
    });
    expect(sunSlots).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import {
  BOOKING_CFG, durationLabel, generateSlots, genCode, sanitizeFirstName, hostLocalToUtc,
} from "./bookingSlots";

// Seeded Eastern availability (from Joshua's real hours).
const EASTERN = "America/Toronto";
const RANGES = {
  1: [{ startMin: 14 * 60, endMin: 20 * 60 }],       // Mon 2:00pm-8:00pm
  2: [{ startMin: 13 * 60, endMin: 19 * 60 + 30 }],  // Tue 1:00pm-7:30pm
  6: [{ startMin: 14 * 60, endMin: 17 * 60 }],       // Sat 2:00pm-5:00pm
};

// A fixed "now": 2026-07-01T12:00:00Z (well clear of DST edges), so lead=24h.
const NOW = Date.UTC(2026, 6, 1, 12, 0, 0);

describe("durationLabel", () => {
  it("formats the 70-minute session as 1 hr 10 min", () => {
    expect(durationLabel(70)).toBe("1 hr 10 min");
    expect(durationLabel(60)).toBe("1 hr");
    expect(durationLabel(30)).toBe("30 min");
  });
});

describe("DST correctness", () => {
  it("US spring-forward (Mar 8 2026) still yields correctly-spaced Eastern slots", () => {
    // now = Mar 1 2026; window includes the DST transition on Mar 8.
    const now = Date.UTC(2026, 2, 1, 12, 0, 0);
    const slots = generateSlots({
      sessionMin: 70, nowMs: now, hostTz: EASTERN,
      rangesByDow: { 0: [{ startMin: 14 * 60, endMin: 17 * 60 }] }, // Sundays 2-5pm to hit Mar 8
      busy: [], daysAhead: 14,
    });
    // Every Sunday slot must still read as :00/:30 in Eastern despite the offset shift.
    for (const s of slots) {
      const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { timeZone: EASTERN, minute: "2-digit", hour12: false }).formatToParts(new Date(s.start_at));
      expect([0, 30]).toContain(Number(parts.find((p) => p.type === "minute")?.value));
    }
  });

  it("US fall-back still yields one unique wall-clock grid", () => {
    const now = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 25, 12, 0, 0);
    const slots = generateSlots({
      sessionMin: 70, nowMs: now, hostTz: EASTERN,
      rangesByDow: { 0: [{ startMin: 14 * 60, endMin: 17 * 60 }] },
      busy: [], daysAhead: 14,
    });
    expect(new Set(slots.map((slot) => slot.start_at)).size).toBe(slots.length);
    for (const slot of slots) {
      const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { timeZone: EASTERN, minute: "2-digit", hour12: false })
        .formatToParts(new Date(slot.start_at));
      expect([0, 30]).toContain(Number(parts.find((part) => part.type === "minute")?.value));
    }
  });
});

19:20 would end 20:30.\n    expect([...new Set(monday)]).toEqual([14 * 60, 15 * 60 + 20, 16 * 60 + 40, 18 * 60]);\n    const tuesday = slots.filter((s) => weekdayOf(s.start_at) === \"Tue\").map((s) => localMinutes(s.start_at));\n    // Tue 13:00-19:30 -> 13:00, 14:20, 15:40, 17:00, 18:20 (ends 19:30 exactly).\n    expect([...new Set(tuesday)]).toEqual([13 * 60, 14 * 60 + 20, 15 * 60 + 40, 17 * 60, 18 * 60 + 20]);\n  });\n\n  it(\"back-to-back slots are all bookable together (one buffer between them)\", () => {\n    // Book the 14:00 Monday slot; import {
  BOOKING_CFG,
  deterministicGoogleEventId,
  generateSlots,
  type BookingPass,
} from "./bookingCore.ts";

function assert(condition: unknown, message: string): asserts condition {
  if (!condition) throw new Error(message);
}

const pass: BookingPass = {
  id: "pass", status: "active", total_credits: 5, used_credits: 0,
  session_length_minutes: 70, expires_at: null, student_id: null,
  student_name: "C", student_email: null,
};

Deno.test("deterministic Google event IDs use UUID hex", () => {
  const id = deterministicGoogleEventId("01234567-89ab-cdef-0123-456789abcdef");
  assert(id === "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef", `unexpected id ${id}`);
  assert(/^[0-9a-v]+$/.test(id), "Google event id has invalid characters");
});

Deno.test("13:15 ranges align to the 30-minute wall-clock grid and deduplicate", () => {
  const nowMs = Date.UTC(2026, 6, 1, 12);
  const slots = generateSlots({
    pass,
    nowMs,
    hostTz: "America/Toronto",
    rangesByDow: { 1: [{ startMin: 13 * 60 + 15, endMin: 18 * 60 }, { startMin: 13 * 60 + 30, endMin: 18 * 60 }] },
    busy: [],
    daysAhead: 14,
  });
  assert(slots.length > 0, "expected slots");
  assert(new Set(slots.map((slot) => slot.start_at)).size === slots.length, "duplicate slots");
  for (const slot of slots) {
    const minute = Number(new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
      timeZone: "America/Toronto", minute: "2-digit", hour12: false,
    }).formatToParts(new Date(slot.start_at)).find((part) => part.type === "minute")?.value);
    assert(minute === 0 || minute === BOOKING_CFG.stepMin, `off-grid minute ${minute}`);
  }
});

export type CalendarSlot = { start_at: string; end_at: string };

export const dayKey = (date: Date) => `${date.getFullYear()}-${date.getMonth()}-${date.getDate()}`;

export const startOfDay = (date: Date) => new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), date.getDate());

export function buildMonthCells(anchor: Date): (Date | null)[] {
  const first = new Date(anchor.getFullYear(), anchor.getMonth(), 1);
  const cells: (Date | null)[] = Array.from({ length: first.getDay() }, () => null);
  const count = new Date(anchor.getFullYear(), anchor.getMonth() + 1, 0).getDate();
  for (let day = 1; day <= count; day += 1) cells.push(new Date(anchor.getFullYear(), anchor.getMonth(), day));
  while (cells.length % 7) cells.push(null);
  return cells;
}

export function groupSlotsByDay<T extends CalendarSlot>(slots: T[]): Map<string, T[]> {
  const grouped = new Map<string, T[]>();
  for (const slot of slots) {
    const key = dayKey(new Date(slot.start_at));
    grouped.set(key, [...(grouped.get(key) ?? []), slot]);
  }
  return grouped;
}

export function slotDays(slots: CalendarSlot[], limit: number): Date[] {
  return [...groupSlotsByDay(slots).keys()].slice(0, limit).map((key) => {
    const [year, month, day] = key.split("-").map(Number);
    return new Date(year, month, day);
  });
}

export function formatBookingTime(iso: string, hour12?: boolean) {
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString(undefined, {
    hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit", ...(hour12 === undefined ? {} : { hour12 }),
  });
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

repo_dir="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
pg_bin="/usr/local/opt/postgresql@17/bin"
test_dir="$(mktemp -d -t lsatu-booking-db.XXXXXX)"
port="55439"

cleanup() {
  "$pg_bin/pg_ctl" -D "$test_dir/data" -m immediate stop >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  rm -rf "$test_dir"
}

src/data/guidedDrillsLocalSeed.ts:1283:      "right_md": "Length -- **10 questions, 15 mins** (1.5x = 23, 2x = 30)  \nTypes -- **Role, Method, and Main Conclusion**  \nTests -- **65, 71, and June 2007**  \nLevels -- **1 - 4**\n\n- Try to answer all 10 questions in 15 minutes.\n- Flag a problem every time you feel less confident in your answer or feel like you struggled to get there.\n- When your timer goes off, flip back through the questions via the Results page and take a second look, hit SHOW ATTEMPT, and check out the Explanation (if there is one).\n- Add any takeaways you want to the NOTES window (a movable, resizable popup), by clicking on the icon in the lower left of the page.\n\n---"
src/data/guidedDrillsLocalSeed.ts:1379:      "right_md": "**Length:** 10 questions  \n**Theme:** Conditional Logic  \n**Levels:** 2 - 4  \n**Time:** 15 mins (1.5x = 22.5, 2x = 30)\n\nTry to get through them timed and then review them untimed before you check your original answer and look at the explanation.\n\n_Conditional Logic, by question type:_\n\n- **Principle** (the left side of the Principle matches the Evidence, the right side matches the Conclusion)\n\n- **Parallel** (diagramming these arguments with \"algebraic recipes\" allows us to be more picky and decisive when evaluating answers)\n\n- **Assumption** (the conclusion gives us an A --> Z logic path we want to prove, and the evidence + the correct answer choice assemble together to create that A --> Z path ... or, the evidence will provide a conditional rule that potentially could output the conclusion, but we still need an answer choice to tell us something in order to clearly trigger that rule.)\n\n- **Must Be True / Most Supported** (multiple conditionals often chain together, and/or we can apply a conditional rule to some provided fact, usually because the fact triggers the contrapositive of the rule)\n\n- **Flaw / Parallel Flaw** (Flaw + Conditional Logic usually means the _Necessary vs. Sufficient_ flaw, in which an author presents a conditional relationship in the Evidence but then interprets it using an illegal Negation or Reversal. Much less frequently, the flaw involves messing up Or / And stuff in the rule.)\n\n---"
src/data/guidedDrillsLocalSeed.ts:1868:      "right_md": "# Choose a study goal\n\n_Untimed:_ Focus on perfect accuracy  \n_Timed:_ 15 mins keep-it-moving\n\nFor **Weaken** questions, focus on the _Anti-Conclusion_. After you read the argument, frame your goal by saying:\n\n_Given_ this evidence  \n_How can I still argue_ the anti-conclusion?\n\nFor **Strengthen** questions, remember that when you're dealing with scientific hypotheses, you can strengthen by either _Ruling Out_ an Alternate Hypothesis, or you can _Boost the Plausibility_ of the Author's Hypothesis.\n\nFor **Principle-Strengthen**, focus on finding an answer that works like \"If _evidence_, then _conclusion_\". A lot of answers are easy to eliminate because they aren't rules that are set up to have the conclusion on the right side of the arrow.\n\n---"
src/data/guidedDrillsLocalSeed.ts:2545:      "right_md": "# Try these 9 problems featuring conditional logic.\n\nTry them all within 15 minutes, then circle back and confirm your thinking (untimed) before confirming your answer and reading the explanation, if available.\n\n# CONDITIONAL KEYWORDS\n\n- Put it on the **Left** (sufficient conditions)  \n  if, when, any, each, all, every, the only, whenever, whoever, X's\n\n- Put it on the **Right** (necessary conditions)  \n  only if, only, must, requires, ensures, will, always, implies, presupposes, depends on, guarantees,\n\n- Put negated version of either idea on left (**If-Not**)  \n  unless, without, until, except\n\n- **Negative to Positive** conversions  \n  No A's are B = All A's are ~B  \n  Not all A's are B = Some A's are ~B  \n  Few A's are B = Most A's are ~B\n\n---"
src/data/guidedDrillsLocalSeed.ts:2858:      "left_md": "**LENGTH** -- 15 Q's, 15 minutes (1.5x = 22, 2x = 30)  \n**TYPES** -- Paradox, Flaw, Role, Main Conclusion, Necessary Assumption, Sufficient Assumption, Strengthen, Weaken, Method, and Must Be True.  \n**LEVEL** -- 1\n\nWait a sec -- these are all level 1? I thought this activity was listed as level 5?\n\nYes, because the goal is something a level 5 student needs to work on: be able to quickly take care of easier questions, so that you'll have more time and brainpower in reserves for the harder, more flexible thinking questions in the section.\n\nGiven how many subtle reading traps there are on LSAT, it's sometimes difficult to ever let yourself believe that an answer you find appealing is correct. \"It's never this easy\", we might think. But, sure it is! Sometimes it really is that easy. We need to improve our ability to _let the easy be easy._\n\nAnd we need to develop enough self-trust while we're doing easier problems that we can pick up our pace when possible.",
src/data/guidedDrillsLocalSeed.ts:2859:      "right_md": "Try to get through these 15 questions in 15 mins. Keep yourself going at a brisk pace. When you have a strong idea of what you're looking for (like on Main Conclusion, Role, Sufficient Assumption, Flaw), try to be proactive about scanning for and finding your answer, rather than reading all five choices start to finish.\n\nHowever, on tasks where we compare the impact of new information (like Paradox, Strengthen, Weaken), we may still need to use more time to consider all five answers.\n\nIf your thinking feels unfinished on any of them, flag them as you go (so that you can maintain your brisk pace), and then take a second look at them after the timer goes off before you check your answer / the explanation."
