{"project_id": "53b9a6f2-aba7-4ae4-8dd1-fa5b8aab4d76", "sql": "BEGIN;\n-- Booking policy mirrors Cal.com, and every check reads the same numbers.\n-- (Applied 2026-09-11 from supabase/migrations/20260911000000_booking_policy_mirrors_calcom.sql)\n\nINSERT INTO public.booking_availability_config (id, timezone)\nVALUES (1, 'America/Toronto')\nON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;\n\nALTER TABLE public.booking_availability_config\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS buffer_minutes integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 10,\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS minimum_notice_minutes integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1440,\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS window_days integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 45;\n\nALTER TABLE public.booking_availability_config\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS booking_availability_config_buffer_even;\nALTER TABLE public.booking_availability_config\n  ADD CONSTRAINT booking_availability_config_buffer_even\n  CHECK (buffer_minutes >= 0 AND buffer_minutes % 2 = 0 AND minimum_notice_minutes >= 0 AND window_days BETWEEN 1 AND 365);\n\nUPDATE public.booking_availability_config\nSET buffer_minutes = 10, minimum_notice_minutes = 1440, window_days = 45, updated_at = now()\nWHERE id = 1;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_buffer_minutes()\nRETURNS integer\nLANGUAGE sql STABLE\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $$\n  SELECT COALESCE((SELECT buffer_minutes FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1), 10);\n$$;\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_availability_overrides (\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n  date date NOT NULL,\n  start_time time,\n  end_time time,\n  note text,\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  CONSTRAINT booking_availability_overrides_times CHECK (\n    (start_time IS NULL AND end_time IS NULL)\n    OR (start_time IS NOT NULL AND end_time IS NOT NULL AND end_time > start_time)\n  )\n);\nCREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS booking_availability_overrides_date_start\n  ON public.booking_availability_overrides (date, COALESCE(start_time, '00:00'::time));\n\nALTER TABLE public.booking_availability_overrides ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"Admins manage availability overrides\" ON public.booking_availability_overrides;\nCREATE POLICY \"Admins manage availability overrides\" ON public.booking_availability_overrides\n  FOR ALL USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'::app_role))\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'::app_role));\n\nINSERT INTO public.booking_availability_overrides (date, start_time, end_time, note)\nVALUES ('2026-09-19', '20:00', '21:10', 'Mirrors the Cal.com date override for Fernande')\nON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_ranges_for_date(_date date)\nRETURNS TABLE(start_time time, end_time time)\nLANGUAGE sql STABLE\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $$\n  WITH o AS (\n    SELECT start_time, end_time FROM public.booking_availability_overrides WHERE date = _date\n  )\n  SELECT start_time, end_time FROM o WHERE start_time IS NOT NULL\n  UNION ALL\n  SELECT a.start_time, a.end_time FROM public.booking_availability a\n  WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM o)\n    AND a.day_of_week = extract(dow FROM _date)::integer;\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_booking_availability()\nRETURNS jsonb\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _cfg public.booking_availability_config%ROWTYPE;\n  _result jsonb;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _cfg FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1;\n  _result := jsonb_build_object(\n    'timezone', COALESCE(_cfg.timezone, 'America/Toronto'),\n    'buffer_minutes', COALESCE(_cfg.buffer_minutes, 10),\n    'minimum_notice_minutes', COALESCE(_cfg.minimum_notice_minutes, 1440),\n    'window_days', COALESCE(_cfg.window_days, 45),\n    'ranges', COALESCE((\n      SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(r) ORDER BY r.day_of_week, r.start_time)\n      FROM (\n        SELECT day_of_week,\n               to_char(start_time, 'HH24:MI') AS start_time,\n               to_char(end_time,   'HH24:MI') AS end_time\n        FROM public.booking_availability\n      ) r\n    ), '[]'::jsonb),\n    'overrides', COALESCE((\n      SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(o) ORDER BY o.date, o.start_time)\n      FROM (\n        SELECT to_char(date, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS date,\n               to_char(start_time, 'HH24:MI') AS start_time,\n               to_char(end_time,   'HH24:MI') AS end_time\n        FROM public.booking_availability_overrides\n        WHERE date >= (now() AT TIME ZONE COALESCE(_cfg.timezone, 'America/Toronto'))::date - 1\n      ) o\n    ), '[]'::jsonb)\n  );\n  RETURN _result;\nEND;\n$function$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.set_booking_availability_overrides(_overrides jsonb)\nRETURNS jsonb\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _tz text := COALESCE((SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1), 'America/Toronto');\n  _today date := (now() AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date;\nBEGIN\n  IF jsonb_typeof(COALESCE(_overrides, '[]'::jsonb)) <> 'array' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_overrides' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n  IF EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(_overrides, '[]'::jsonb)) e\n    WHERE (e->>'date') IS NULL\n       OR ((e->>'start_time') IS NULL) <> ((e->>'end_time') IS NULL)\n       OR ((e->>'start_time') IS NOT NULL AND (e->>'end_time')::time <= (e->>'start_time')::time)\n  ) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_override_range' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n  DELETE FROM public.booking_availability_overrides WHERE date >= _today;\n  INSERT INTO public.booking_availability_overrides (date, start_time, end_time, note)\n  SELECT (e->>'date')::date, (e->>'start_time')::time, (e->>'end_time')::time, e->>'note'\n  FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(_overrides, '[]'::jsonb)) e\n  WHERE (e->>'date')::date >= _today;\n  RETURN public.get_booking_availability();\nEND;\n$function$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_slot_is_clear(\n  _start_at timestamptz,\n  _end_at timestamptz,\n  _buffer_before_minutes integer,\n  _buffer_after_minutes integer,\n  _ignore_single_session_id uuid DEFAULT NULL,\n  _ignore_google_event_id text DEFAULT NULL,\n  _ignore_schedule_event_id uuid DEFAULT NULL\n)\nRETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $$\n  WITH probe AS (\n    SELECT tstzrange(\n      _start_at - make_interval(mins => GREATEST(_buffer_before_minutes, 0)),\n      _end_at   + make_interval(mins => GREATEST(_buffer_after_minutes, 0)),\n      '[)'\n    ) AS r\n  )\n  SELECT NOT EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM public.single_session_bookings b, probe\n    WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open', 'booked')\n      AND (_ignore_single_session_id IS NULL OR b.id <> _ignore_single_session_id)\n      AND tstzrange(b.start_at, b.end_at, '[)') && probe.r\n  ) AND NOT EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions x, probe\n    WHERE x.status = 'booked'\n      AND tstzrange(x.start_at, x.end_at, '[)') && probe.r\n  ) AND NOT EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM public.schedule_events se, probe\n    WHERE se.deleted_at IS NULL\n      AND se.status <> 'cancelled'\n      AND COALESCE(se.transparency, 'opaque') <> 'transparent'\n      AND (_ignore_google_event_id IS NULL OR se.google_event_id IS DISTINCT FROM _ignore_google_event_id)\n      AND (_ignore_schedule_event_id IS NULL OR se.id <> _ignore_schedule_event_id)\n      AND tstzrange(se.start_at, se.end_at, '[)') && probe.r\n  );\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_buffered_range(_s timestamptz, _e timestamptz)\nRETURNS tstzrange\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE\nSET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'\nAS $$\n  SELECT tstzrange(_s - interval '5 minutes', _e + interval '5 minutes', '[)');\n$$;\n\nALTER TABLE public.booking_pass_redemptions DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS no_overlapping_bookings;\nALTER TABLE public.booking_pass_redemptions ADD CONSTRAINT no_overlapping_bookings\n  EXCLUDE USING gist (public.booking_buffered_range(start_at, end_at) WITH &&)\n  WHERE (status = 'booked');\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_slot_bookable(_start_at timestamptz, _len integer)\nRETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _cfg        public.booking_availability_config%ROWTYPE;\n  _tz         text;\n  _buffer     integer;\n  _pitch      integer;\n  _end_at     timestamptz;\n  _local      timestamp;\n  _local_date date;\n  _local_time time;\n  _r          record;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _cfg FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1;\n  _tz     := COALESCE(_cfg.timezone, 'America/Toronto');\n  _buffer := COALESCE(_cfg.buffer_minutes, 10);\n  _pitch  := _len + _buffer;\n\n  IF _start_at IS NULL OR _len IS NULL OR _len <= 0 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF EXTRACT(SECOND FROM _start_at)::int <> 0 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF _start_at < now() + make_interval(mins => COALESCE(_cfg.minimum_notice_minutes, 1440)) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n  IF _start_at > now() + make_interval(days => COALESCE(_cfg.window_days, 45)) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  _end_at     := _start_at + make_interval(mins => _len);\n  _local      := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;\n  _local_date := _local::date;\n  _local_time := _local::time;\n\n  FOR _r IN SELECT * FROM public.booking_ranges_for_date(_local_date) LOOP\n    IF _local_time >= _r.start_time\n       AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _len) <= _r.end_time\n       AND mod(floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - _r.start_time)) / 60)::integer, _pitch) = 0\n    THEN\n      RETURN true;\n    END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  RETURN false;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND;\n$function$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.redeem_booking_pass_v2(\n  _pass_id uuid, _idempotency_key text, _start_at timestamptz,\n  _schedule_event_id uuid, _student_name text, _student_email text\n)\nRETURNS TABLE(redemption_id uuid, start_at timestamptz, end_at timestamptz, used_credits integer, total_credits integer, status text, was_existing boolean)\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _pass public.booking_passes%ROWTYPE;\n  _existing public.booking_pass_redemptions%ROWTYPE;\n  _end_at timestamptz;\n  _new_id uuid;\n  _buffer integer := public.booking_buffer_minutes();\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _existing FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions\n    WHERE booking_pass_id = _pass_id AND idempotency_key = _idempotency_key;\n  IF FOUND THEN\n    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_existing.start_at));\n    SELECT * INTO _pass FROM public.booking_passes WHERE id = _pass_id;\n    redemption_id := _existing.id; start_at := _existing.start_at; end_at := _existing.end_at;\n    used_credits := _pass.used_credits; total_credits := _pass.total_credits;\n    status := _pass.status; was_existing := true; RETURN NEXT; RETURN;\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT * INTO _pass FROM public.booking_passes WHERE id = _pass_id FOR UPDATE;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'pass_not_found'; END IF;\n  SELECT * INTO _existing FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions\n    WHERE booking_pass_id = _pass_id AND idempotency_key = _idempotency_key;\n  IF FOUND THEN\n    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_existing.start_at));\n    redemption_id := _existing.id; start_at := _existing.start_at; end_at := _existing.end_at;\n    used_credits := _pass.used_credits; total_credits := _pass.total_credits;\n    status := _pass.status; was_existing := true; RETURN NEXT; RETURN;\n  END IF;\n  IF _pass.status <> 'active' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'pass_not_active:%', _pass.status; END IF;\n  IF _pass.expires_at IS NOT NULL AND _pass.expires_at < now() THEN\n    UPDATE public.booking_passes SET status = 'expired' WHERE id = _pass_id;\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'pass_expired';\n  END IF;\n  IF _pass.used_credits >= _pass.total_credits THEN\n    UPDATE public.booking_passes SET status = 'exhausted' WHERE id = _pass_id;\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'pass_exhausted';\n  END IF;\n  _end_at := _start_at + make_interval(mins => _pass.session_length_minutes);\n  IF NOT public.is_slot_bookable(_start_at, _pass.session_length_minutes) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_unavailable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0425';\n  END IF;\n\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_start_at));\n  IF NOT public.booking_slot_is_clear(_start_at, _end_at, _buffer, _buffer, NULL, NULL, _schedule_event_id) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_conflict' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  BEGIN\n    INSERT INTO public.booking_pass_redemptions (\n      booking_pass_id, schedule_event_id, google_event_id, start_at, end_at,\n      status, idempotency_key, student_name_snapshot, student_email_snapshot,\n      gcal_state, needs_gcal_sync\n    ) VALUES (\n      _pass_id, _schedule_event_id, NULL, _start_at, _end_at, 'booked',\n      _idempotency_key, _student_name, _student_email, 'pending', true\n    ) RETURNING id INTO _new_id;\n  EXCEPTION WHEN unique_violation THEN\n    SELECT * INTO _existing FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions\n      WHERE booking_pass_id = _pass_id AND idempotency_key = _idempotency_key;\n    redemption_id := _existing.id; start_at := _existing.start_at; end_at := _existing.end_at;\n    used_credits := _pass.used_credits; total_credits := _pass.total_credits;\n    status := _pass.status; was_existing := true; RETURN NEXT; RETURN;\n  END;\n  UPDATE public.booking_passes bp SET\n    used_credits = bp.used_credits + 1,\n    status = CASE WHEN bp.used_credits + 1 >= bp.total_credits THEN 'exhausted' ELSE 'active' END\n  WHERE bp.id = _pass_id RETURNING * INTO _pass;\n  redemption_id := _new_id; start_at := _start_at; end_at := _end_at;\n  used_credits := _pass.used_credits; total_credits := _pass.total_credits;\n  status := _pass.status; was_existing := false; RETURN NEXT;\nEND;\n$function$;\n\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS window_calendar_days integer;\n\nUPDATE public.booking_event_types\nSET buffer_before_minutes = 10, buffer_after_minutes = 10,\n    slot_interval_minutes = 80, window_calendar_days = 45, updated_at = now()\nWHERE slug = 'single-session';\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now timestamptz DEFAULT now(), _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'::text)\nRETURNS date\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\n    'America/Toronto'\n  );\n  _day date := (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date;\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n  _target integer;\n  _business_days integer := 0;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug;\n  IF FOUND AND _event.window_calendar_days IS NOT NULL THEN\n    RETURN _day + _event.window_calendar_days;\n  END IF;\n  _target := COALESCE(_event.window_business_days, 6);\n  WHILE _business_days < _target LOOP\n    IF extract(isodow FROM _day) BETWEEN 1 AND 5 THEN\n      _business_days := _business_days + 1;\n    END IF;\n    IF _business_days < _target THEN _day := _day + 1; END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  RETURN _day;\nEND;\n$function$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(_start_at timestamptz, _now timestamptz DEFAULT now(), _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'::text)\nRETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\n    'America/Toronto'\n  );\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n  _length integer;\n  _pitch integer;\n  _local_start timestamp;\n  _local_date date;\n  _local_time time;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug AND active;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF _start_at IS NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  IF _event.pinned_start_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    RETURN _start_at = _event.pinned_start_at AND _start_at > _now;\n  END IF;\n\n  _length := _event.length_minutes;\n  _pitch := COALESCE(_event.slot_interval_minutes, _event.length_minutes);\n\n  IF _start_at < _now + make_interval(mins => _event.minimum_notice_minutes) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  _local_start := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;\n  _local_date := _local_start::date;\n  _local_time := _local_start::time;\n\n  IF _local_date < (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date\n     OR _local_date > public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now, _event_slug) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1\n    FROM public.booking_ranges_for_date(_local_date) a\n    WHERE _local_time >= a.start_time\n      AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _length) <= a.end_time\n      AND mod(\n        floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - a.start_time)) / 60)::integer,\n        _pitch\n      ) = 0\n  );\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND;\n$function$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_slot(_start_at timestamptz, _name text, _email text, _notes text, _guests jsonb, _client_idempotency_key text, _booking_token_hash text, _ip_hash text, _user_id uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'::text)\nRETURNS single_session_bookings\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n  _length integer;\n  _end_at timestamptz;\n  _email_normalized text := lower(trim(_email));\n  _guests_normalized jsonb;\n  _expired record;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug;\n  _length := COALESCE(_event.length_minutes, 70);\n  _end_at := _start_at + make_interval(mins => _length);\n\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings\n  WHERE client_idempotency_key = _client_idempotency_key;\n  IF FOUND THEN\n    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_booking.start_at));\n    SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking.id FOR UPDATE;\n    RETURN _booking;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _start_at IS NULL OR NOT public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(_start_at, now(), _event_slug) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_unavailable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0425';\n  END IF;\n  IF NULLIF(trim(_name), '') IS NULL OR length(trim(_name)) > 200 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_name' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n  IF _email_normalized !~ '^[^@[:space:]]+@[^@[:space:]]+[.][^@[:space:]]+$'\n     OR length(_email_normalized) > 320 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_email' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n  IF length(COALESCE(_notes, '')) > 4000 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'notes_too_long' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n  IF length(COALESCE(_client_idempotency_key, '')) NOT BETWEEN 8 AND 200\n     OR length(COALESCE(_booking_token_hash, '')) NOT BETWEEN 16 AND 256\n     OR length(COALESCE(_ip_hash, '')) NOT BETWEEN 16 AND 256 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_capability' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(COALESCE(_guests, '[]'::jsonb)) <> 'array'\n     OR jsonb_array_length(COALESCE(_guests, '[]'::jsonb)) > 10 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_guests' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(g.email) ORDER BY g.email), '[]'::jsonb)\n  INTO _guests_normalized\n  FROM (\n    SELECT DISTINCT lower(trim(value #>> '{}')) AS email\n    FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(_guests, '[]'::jsonb))\n    WHERE jsonb_typeof(value) = 'string'\n      AND lower(trim(value #>> '{}')) <> _email_normalized\n      AND lower(trim(value #>> '{}')) ~ '^[^@[:space:]]+@[^@[:space:]]+[.][^@[:space:]]+$'\n  ) g;\n  IF jsonb_array_length(_guests_normalized) <> jsonb_array_length(COALESCE(_guests, '[]'::jsonb)) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_or_duplicate_guests' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_start_at));\n\n  FOR _expired IN\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b\n      SET status = 'expired', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n          terminal_reason = 'authoritative_expiry'\n    WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open')\n      AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) <= now()\n      AND tstzrange(b.start_at, b.end_at, '[)') && tstzrange(_start_at, _end_at, '[)')\n    RETURNING b.id, b.lifecycle_version, b.gcal_event_id\n  LOOP\n    IF _expired.gcal_event_id IS NOT NULL THEN\n      PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(\n        _expired.id, 'gcal_delete', 'system', 'pending', 'expired',\n        _expired.lifecycle_version, NULL\n      );\n    END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n\n  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings b\n      WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open')\n        AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) > now()\n        AND b.ip_hash = _ip_hash) >= 3 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hold_cap_ip' USING ERRCODE = 'P0429';\n  END IF;\n  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings b\n      WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open')\n        AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) > now()\n        AND lower(b.email) = _email_normalized) >= 3 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hold_cap_email' USING ERRCODE = 'P0429';\n  END IF;\n  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings b\n      WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open')\n        AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) > now()) >= 250 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hold_cap_global' USING ERRCODE = 'P0429';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NOT public.booking_slot_is_clear(\n    _start_at, _end_at,\n    COALESCE(_event.buffer_before_minutes, 0), COALESCE(_event.buffer_after_minutes, 0)\n  ) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_conflict' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  INSERT INTO public.single_session_bookings (\n    start_at, end_at, name, email, notes, guests, client_idempotency_key,\n    draft_expires_at, user_id, booking_token_hash, booking_token_expires_at, ip_hash,\n    event_slug\n  ) VALUES (\n    _start_at, _end_at, trim(_name), _email_normalized, NULLIF(trim(_notes), ''),\n    _guests_normalized, _client_idempotency_key, now() + interval '5 minutes',\n    _user_id, _booking_token_hash, now() + interval '24 hours', _ip_hash,\n    _event_slug\n  ) RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n\n  RETURN _booking;\nEXCEPTION WHEN exclusion_violation OR unique_violation THEN\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings\n  WHERE client_idempotency_key = _client_idempotency_key;\n  IF FOUND THEN RETURN _booking; END IF;\n  RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_conflict' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\nEND;\n$function$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_free(_booking_id uuid, _expected_version integer, _live_calendar_clear boolean, _cancel_token_hash text, _cancel_payload_ciphertext text)\nRETURNS jsonb\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n  _can_book boolean := false;\n  _new_version integer;\n  _slot_start timestamptz;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT start_at INTO _slot_start FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'booking_not_found' USING ERRCODE = 'P0404'; END IF;\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_slot_start));\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id FOR UPDATE;\n\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _booking.event_slug;\n  IF _event.requires_payment IS DISTINCT FROM false THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_required' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _booking.status = 'booked' THEN\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n      'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', true);\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _booking.status <> 'draft' OR _booking.lifecycle_version <> _expected_version THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'stale_booking' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NULLIF(_cancel_token_hash, '') IS NULL OR NULLIF(_cancel_payload_ciphertext, '') IS NULL THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'cancel_capability_required' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n\n  _can_book := _live_calendar_clear AND public.booking_slot_is_clear(\n    _booking.start_at, _booking.end_at,\n    COALESCE(_event.buffer_before_minutes, 0), COALESCE(_event.buffer_after_minutes, 0),\n    _booking.id, _booking.gcal_event_id, NULL\n  );\n\n  IF NOT _can_book THEN\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\n      status = 'expired', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n      terminal_reason = 'slot_taken_before_confirm'\n    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n    IF _booking.gcal_event_id IS NOT NULL THEN\n      PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_delete', 'system', 'pending',\n        'expired', _booking.lifecycle_version, NULL);\n    END IF;\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n      'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', false);\n  END IF;\n\n  UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\n    status = 'booked', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n    amount_cents = 0, currency = 'usd',\n    cancel_token_hash = _cancel_token_hash, cancel_token_expires_at = b.start_at,\n    terminal_reason = NULL\n  WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n  _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\n\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_promote', 'system', 'pending',\n    'booked', _new_version, NULL);\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_booking_confirmation', 'booker', 'blocked',\n    'booked', _new_version, _cancel_payload_ciphertext);\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_admin_notification', 'admin', 'blocked',\n    'booked', _new_version, NULL);\n\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n    'lifecycle_version', _new_version, 'was_existing', false);\nEND;\n$function$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_payment(_booking_id uuid, _provider_event_id text, _stripe_checkout_session_id text, _stripe_payment_intent_id text, _amount_cents integer, _currency text, _livemode boolean, _live_calendar_clear boolean, _cancel_token_hash text, _cancel_payload_ciphertext text)\nRETURNS jsonb\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n  _claimed_booking_id uuid;\n  _can_book boolean := false;\n  _new_version integer;\n  _slot_start timestamptz;\nBEGIN\n  IF NULLIF(_provider_event_id, '') IS NULL\n     OR NULLIF(_stripe_checkout_session_id, '') IS NULL\n     OR NULLIF(_stripe_payment_intent_id, '') IS NULL\n     OR _amount_cents <> 4500 OR lower(COALESCE(_currency, '')) <> 'usd'\n     OR _livemode IS NULL THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_payment_facts' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT start_at INTO _slot_start FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'booking_not_found' USING ERRCODE = 'P0404'; END IF;\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_slot_start));\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id FOR UPDATE;\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _booking.event_slug;\n\n  SELECT single_session_booking_id INTO _claimed_booking_id\n  FROM public.booking_commerce_events\n  WHERE provider = 'stripe' AND provider_event_id = _provider_event_id\n    AND event_type = 'single_session_paid';\n  IF FOUND AND _claimed_booking_id IS DISTINCT FROM _booking_id THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'provider_event_already_claimed' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _booking.stripe_checkout_session_id IS DISTINCT FROM _stripe_checkout_session_id THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'checkout_mismatch' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _booking.status IN ('booked', 'cancelled', 'refund_pending', 'refunded', 'refund_failed') THEN\n    IF _booking.stripe_payment_intent_id IS DISTINCT FROM _stripe_payment_intent_id\n       OR _booking.amount_cents IS DISTINCT FROM _amount_cents\n       OR lower(_booking.currency) IS DISTINCT FROM lower(_currency)\n       OR _booking.livemode IS DISTINCT FROM _livemode THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_facts_mismatch' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n    END IF;\n    INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (\n      provider, provider_event_id, event_type, offer_id, amount_cents, currency,\n      payload, single_session_booking_id\n    ) VALUES (\n      'stripe', _provider_event_id, 'single_session_paid', 'single-session-native',\n      _amount_cents, lower(_currency),\n      jsonb_build_object('livemode', _livemode, 'checkout_session_id', _stripe_checkout_session_id),\n      _booking_id\n    ) ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n      'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', true);\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _booking.status NOT IN ('checkout_open', 'expired', 'abandoned') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_not_finalizable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n  END IF;\n\n  INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (\n    provider, provider_event_id, event_type, offer_id, amount_cents, currency,\n    payload, single_session_booking_id\n  ) VALUES (\n    'stripe', _provider_event_id, 'single_session_paid', 'single-session-native',\n    _amount_cents, lower(_currency),\n    jsonb_build_object('livemode', _livemode, 'checkout_session_id', _stripe_checkout_session_id),\n    _booking_id\n  ) ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\n\n  _can_book := _live_calendar_clear\n    AND (_booking.status = 'checkout_open'\n      OR (_booking.status = 'expired' AND _booking.status_changed_at >= now() - interval '10 minutes'))\n    AND public.booking_slot_is_clear(\n      _booking.start_at, _booking.end_at,\n      COALESCE(_event.buffer_before_minutes, 0), COALESCE(_event.buffer_after_minutes, 0),\n      _booking.id, _booking.gcal_event_id, NULL\n    );\n\n  IF _can_book THEN\n    IF NULLIF(_cancel_token_hash, '') IS NULL OR NULLIF(_cancel_payload_ciphertext, '') IS NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'cancel_capability_required' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n    END IF;\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\n      status = 'booked', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n      stripe_payment_intent_id = _stripe_payment_intent_id,\n      amount_cents = _amount_cents, currency = lower(_currency), livemode = _livemode,\n      cancel_token_hash = _cancel_token_hash, cancel_token_expires_at = b.start_at,\n      terminal_reason = NULL\n    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n    _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\n\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_promote', 'system', 'pending',\n      'booked', _new_version, NULL);\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_booking_confirmation', 'booker', 'blocked',\n      'booked', _new_version, _cancel_payload_ciphertext);\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_admin_notification', 'admin', 'blocked',\n      'booked', _new_version, NULL);\n  ELSE\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\n      status = 'refund_pending', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n      stripe_payment_intent_id = _stripe_payment_intent_id,\n      amount_cents = _amount_cents, currency = lower(_currency), livemode = _livemode,\n      terminal_reason = 'paid_slot_unavailable'\n    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n    _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\n    UPDATE public.single_session_outbox SET state = 'superseded', updated_at = now()\n      WHERE booking_id = _booking.id AND state IN ('blocked', 'pending', 'failed')\n        AND action IN ('gcal_promote', 'email_booking_confirmation', 'email_admin_notification');\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_delete', 'system', 'pending',\n      'refund_pending', _new_version, NULL);\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'stripe_refund', 'system', 'pending',\n      'refund_pending', _new_version, NULL);\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n    'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', false);\nEND;\n$function$;\n\nUPDATE public.schedule_events\nSET deleted_at = now(), status = 'cancelled'\nWHERE deleted_at IS NULL\n  AND source = 'google'\n  AND google_event_id IN ('4gehvktovf170bsvkju1d2utuq_20260912T184500Z', '4gehvktovf170bsvkju1d2utuq_20260919T184500Z');\n\nCOMMIT;"}

The consultation keeps Cal.com's 30-minute pitch. Every page that offers a Slot and every check that accepts one uses the same arithmetic; a Slot a student can see is a Slot they can book.\"\"\")\ns=s.replace(\"\"\"### Buffer\nThe gap Joshua requires between the end of one calendar event and the start of the next: **10 minutes**, counted once between any two events regardless of which of them was booked where. A Slot is offered only if the whole session plus the Buffer fits clear of everything else, and every check that decides whether a Slot is free uses this same number. _Avoid: padding, breathing room, \"15 minutes\" (the old number)._\n\n### Booking Window\nHow far into the future slots are offered. The Single Session mirrors Cal.com: 6 business days out, non-rolling.\"\"\",\"\"\"### Buffer\nThe gap Joshua requires between the end of one calendar event and the start of the next: **10 minutes**, counted once between any two events regardless of which of them was booked where. A Slot is offered only if the whole session plus the Buffer fits clear of everything else, and every check that decides whether a Slot is free uses this same number. The consultation carries its own larger buffer, as it does on Cal.com. _Avoid: padding, breathing room, \"15 minutes\" (the old number)._\n\n### Date Override\nHours for one specific date that replace the weekly hours on that date, exactly as Cal.com's date overrides do. An override with no hours closes the day. Used for one-off availability (a Saturday evening for one student) or a day off.\n\n### Booking Window\nHow far into the future slots are offered. Sessions, whether from a Booking Pass or a Single Session, mirror Cal.com's session link: **45 calendar days**, rolling. The consultation keeps its 5-business-day window.\"\"\")\nopen(p,'w',encoding='utf-8',newline='\\n').write(s); print('ok')\nPYEOF\ngrep -c \"Date Override\" src/components/booking/CONTEXT.md", "description": "Update the booking glossary terms"}

One Buffer, counted once, read from one row\n\nDate: 2026-09-11\n\n## Status\n\nAccepted\n\n## Context\n\nFour places decided whether a booking slot was free, each with its own arithmetic. The slot list and the edge-function guard required a 15-minute gap next to an existing event. `redeem_booking_pass_v2` and the `no_overlapping_bookings` exclusion constraint buffered *both* rows, so two pass bookings needed 30 minutes of clearance. The three single-session RPCs used yet another mix (no buffer against calendar events, 15 against pass bookings).\n\nOn 2026-09-10 a student tried five times to book from her pass link and was told \"That time was just taken\" every time. Nothing was taken: 15 of the 109 slots she could see were offered by the list and refused by the database. Cal.com, which the booking pages are meant to mirror, runs the session-link event with 0 buffer and a 70-minute pitch.\n\n## Decision\n\n- The Buffer is **10 minutes** (Joshua's number), stored once in `booking_availability_config.buffer_minutes` alongside the notice and the window. Every SQL check reads it through `booking_buffer_minutes()`; every edge function reads it through `get_booking_availability()`.\n- The Buffer is counted **once** between any two events. Busy rows are never buffered themselves; only the probe is.\n- One predicate, `booking_slot_is_clear()`, answers \"is anything in this database in the way?\" for the pass RPC and all three single-session RPCs. Single-session events pass their own per-event buffers (the consultation keeps 30/30 to mirror Cal.com).\n- The exclusion constraint buffers each row by **half** the Buffer (a literal 5 minutes), so two pass bookings need exactly one Buffer between them. GiST requires an IMMUTABLE expression, so this is the one place the number is copied rather than read.\n- Slot pitch is session length + Buffer (80 minutes), so consecutive grid slots are all bookable together instead of a booking wasting the slots on either side.\n\n## Consequences\n\n- A slot a student can see is a slot the database will accept. The Deno tests pin the generator, and the live database was checked against every generated slot on 2026-09-11 (134/134 pass link, 134/134 single session, 26/26 consultation).\n- Changing the Buffer means updating the config row **and** re-creating `booking_buffered_range` with half the new value, then rebuilding `no_overlapping_bookings`. The migration that introduced this says so at the bottom.\n- Cal.com's session-link event still runs at 0 buffer. Until it is set to 10 there, a booking made through Cal.com can sit closer to an LSAT U booking than LSAT U itself would allow.\n"}

{"file_path": "C:\\Users\\Joshu\\.claude\\projects\\C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os\\memory\\booking-slot-checks-must-share-one-arithmetic.md", "content": "---\nname: booking-slot-checks-must-share-one-arithmetic\ndescription: \"That time was just taken\" with nothing taken means the slot list and the DB predicate disagree; every LSAT U slot check reads booking_availability_config (buffer 10, notice 1440, window 45) and the shared booking_slot_is_clear().\nmetadata:\n  type: project\n---\n\nOn 2026-09-10 Sandra hit \"That time was just taken\" five times from her pass link. Zero\nredemptions existed. Cause: the slot list and the edge guard required a 15-minute gap next to\nan existing booking, but `redeem_booking_pass_v2` and the `no_overlapping_bookings` EXCLUDE\nconstraint buffered both rows (30 min effective). 15 of her 109 visible slots were traps: the\nfirst slot after and last slot before each existing session.\n\nFixed 2026-09-11 (PR #115, migration `20260911000000_booking_policy_mirrors_calcom.sql`,\nADR `docs/adr/0010-one-buffer-counted-once.md`):\n- Buffer **10 min** (Joshua's number), notice 1440, window 45 calendar days, all in\n  `booking_availability_config` row 1. SQL reads it via `booking_buffer_minutes()`; edge code\n  via `get_booking_availability()` -> `parseAvailability()` / `loadAvailability()`.\n- One predicate `booking_slot_is_clear(start, end, before, after, ...)` used by the pass RPC\n  and all three single-session RPCs. Busy rows are never buffered; only the probe is.\n- EXCLUDE constraint buffers each row by a literal **5 min** (GiST needs IMMUTABLE). Changing\n  the buffer means re-creating `booking_buffered_range` and rebuilding the constraint.\n- One `generateSlots({ policy, hours, nowMs, busy })` in `_shared/bookingCore.ts`; the\n  single session and consultation delegate to it. Pitch = length + buffer (80 min), stepping\n  from each range start. `is_slot_bookable` enforces the same grid.\n- Date overrides live in `booking_availability_overrides` (a date's rows replace weekly\n  hours; null times close the day). Admin write path `set_booking_availability_overrides`.\n  No admin UI yet.\n\n**Why:** Two independent implementations of \"is this slot free?\" will drift. ## assistant

Everything is live and verified. Here's the full account.

## What Sandra hit

Her `/book/sandra-…` pass link (LSAT U, not Cal.com). Five attempts: one on 2026-09-09 00:15 ET, four between 21:54 and 21:55 ET on 2026-09-10. **None went through; nothing was taken; her 10 credits are untouched.**

The slot list required a 15-minute gap next to an existing booking. The database RPC and its exclusion constraint buffered *both* rows, so a pass booking needed 30 minutes from another pass booking. The list offered 15:30 on Mon 09-14 (Charles ends 15:10); the database refused it. **15 of the 109 slots she could see were traps**, always the first slot after and last slot before one of your sessions, which is exactly where a student looking for the next available time clicks. It had been that way since 2026-07-19.

A second, unrelated bug was hiding both Saturdays: two phantom "Andrew" rows synced on 2026-06-22 from a recurring event you've since removed from Google. The mirror never marked them deleted, so 09-12 and 09-19 showed nothing while Cal.com offered them.

## What's fixed (PR [#115](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/pull/115), merged, migration applied, four edge functions deployed)

| | Before | Now (mirrors Cal.com `session-link`) |
|---|---|---|
| Buffer | 15 / 30 / 0, depending on which code path | **10 min**, one number, one config row, counted once |
| Slot pitch | every 30 min | **80 min** (70 + 10) from each range start |
| Window | 21 days | **45 calendar days**, rolling |
| Date overrides | none | supported; 09-19 20:00–21:10 seeded from Cal.com |
| Stale calendar rows | kept forever | tombstoned after each clean sync; the two Andrew rows cleared |
| Overlap check | four hand-copied SQL clauses | one `booking_slot_is_clear()` used by every booking path |

**Proof, against the live database:** I generated every slot the new code offers and fed each one through the exact SQL the RPC runs. Pass link 134/134 accepted, single session 134/134, consultation 26/26. Then I confirmed the deployed endpoints return byte-identical lists. Saturday 09-12 is open again; Mon 09-14 15:20 (the slot after Charles) is offered.

**Calendar + Meet:** already worked and unchanged: every booking creates a Google event on your calendar with a Meet link, the student as attendee, and invites sent. All 12 upcoming pass bookings show `synced` with a Meet URL. The in-app `/schedule` page uses the same function as the direct link; all 10 active passes are linked to accounts and have slugs.

## What I did not do, and why

- **Cal.com's `session-link` is still at 0 buffer.** You said 10. Setting it on Cal.com changes a live page you've run at 0 for months, so I left it for your say-so; it's one API call.
- **No admin UI for date overrides yet.** They work (SQL or `set_booking_availability_overrides`), but you can't add one from the teacher screen.
- The LSAT U consultation page uses your Working Hours; Cal.com's consultation has its own 7-day 13:30–20:00 schedule. Pre-existing, not changed.
- `scripts/test-booking-db.sh` needs a Mac-path Postgres 17; not run. Five `SingleSession.test.tsx` UI tests fail identically on untouched main (locale-dependent "1:00 PM" lookups).

Runbook: [wiki/LSATU-BOOKING-POLICY.md](wiki/LSATU-BOOKING-POLICY.md). ADR: `docs/adr/0010-one-buffer-counted-once.md`.

**Suggested reply to Sandra (draft, not sent):**

> Hi Sandra, thanks for flagging this and sorry for the runaround. None of your bookings went through, so nothing was charged against your package and you still have all 10 sessions. The error was on my end: the calendar was showing a few times it then refused. That's fixed. Your same link works now, and you'll see times every 80 minutes across the next six weeks. Book whichever suits you and you'll get a calendar invite with the Meet link right away.

## assistant

Two answers.

**Will her link work?** Yes. The same link (`/book/sandra-wc4kt2xjhcbxc4bqbytas9qmad`) now returns 134 slots, every one checked against the live database, and I confirmed the deployed endpoint returns that exact list. Nothing about the link changed; the pass is active with all 10 credits.

**Which times did she try?** The system did not record that. The only trail is the rate-limit log, which counts attempts per minute by action, not the slot chosen:

| When (ET) | What |
|---|---|
| 2026-09-09 00:13 | opened the page (2 slot loads) |
| 2026-09-09 00:15 | **1 booking attempt**, then 3 slot reloads |
| 2026-09-10 21:54 | 6 slot loads, **2 booking attempts** |
| 2026-09-10 21:55 | **2 booking attempts**, 8 slot reloads |
| 2026-09-10 21:57 | emailed you |

What I can infer, not verify: at 21:54 on 09-10 the first day she could see was Mon 09-14 (Fri was inside the 24-hour notice, Sat was hidden by the phantom Andrew row), and the first slot on that day, 15:30, was a trap. The page reloads the list after each failure but the trap stays on it, so retrying the same time fails again. That fits four attempts in two minutes.

So that the next report has an answer, I'll add one line of logging: when a booking is refused, record the slot and the reason. Small change, one more deploy.

## assistant

Not credits. My wording was bad. "10/10" meant **10 minutes before, 10 minutes after**: Cal.com's two buffer settings on an event.

Here's the situation:

- You told me to guarantee a 10-minute gap between sessions. LSAT U now enforces that everywhere.
- Your Cal.com `session-link` event has **0 minutes before / 0 minutes after** ("Before event" and "After event" under the event's Limits tab). So a student booking through Cal.com can still land a session that starts the minute your previous one ends.
- LSAT U sees Cal.com bookings through your Google calendar, so it will keep its own 10 minutes clear of them. The gap only opens in the other direction: Cal.com doesn't know about LSAT U's rule.

The question is just whether you want Cal.com to require the same 10-minute gap. If yes, I can set `beforeEventBuffer: 10` and `afterEventBuffer: 10` on that one event through the API. If you'd rather keep Cal.com as it is, nothing else needs to change.

## user

Tool result:
- **Forward-looking.** End on the next step or what's ahead.
- **No em dashes, ever.** Use periods, colons before lists, the middot (·) as a separator, and numbered steps for procedures.

## Openings, closings, and signature phrases

The mechanical fingerprint of his email, from the real corpus. Match these when drafting as Joshua.

- **Greeting.** "Hi [Name]," is the default (used ~519x). "Hey [Name]," for established/returning students (~229x, warmer). "Hello [Name]," (~105x) and "Dear [Name]," (~18x, formal) for first contact or a more formal note.
- **Sign-off.** "Best," and "Best regards," are the workhorses (~360x / ~237x). "Thanks," when it's a favor or logistics (~146x). "Warmly," / "Warm regards," (~54x) for a genuinely warm note. "Talk soon," (~9x) for someone he'll see again soon.
- **Signature.** Almost always signed **"Joshua"** (844 of 944 messages). The name is near-universal; include it.
- **Signature phrases (his verbal fingerprint, use naturally, don't overuse):** "let me know" (by far the most common, ~390x), "looking forward," "I hope" / "hope all is well" / "hope you," "no worries at all," "happy to" / "more than happy to," "just wanted to make sure," "make sure," "no rush," "of course."
- **Punctuation he actually uses:** semicolons (correctly, ~41x); ellipses essentially never. (For em-dashes, the hard rule in Core voice governs — never.)

## The apology / accountability move

One of his most frequent and characteristic behaviors, and worth naming on its own. When he is late to reply, or missed or mishandled something, he opens by owning it plainly, often with a brief honest reason, then pivots straight to the fix. No over-apologizing, no excuses dressed up.

> I'm so sorry. I thought I had let you know ... I clearly didn't communicate that as well as I should have.

> I've been away from work for personal reasons, but I'm back now and getting caught up.

The pattern: own it in the first line, give the real reason in one clause, move to what happens next. Warm, candid, brief.

## Situational micro-templates

Verbatim-grounded reference lines for the situations that recur most. Adapt, never paste blindly.

- **Student reschedules / cancels:** low-pressure, no guilt.
  > No worries at all. It happens, so please don't worry about it. If you would like to reschedule, just let me know.
- **He missed / no-showed (approved make-good template):** full ownership, real reason, offer to make it right (see the apology move above). When the cause was an unexpected health issue, the approved shape is: sincerely apologize, state that a serious health scare unexpectedly took him out of commission, offer a **completely free, full LSAT session**, and hand them the complimentary booking link `https://cal.com/joshua-jtx5ud/complimentary-session`. Emphasis stays limited: bold the complimentary offer, italicize the line that one focused session can put points on the board. Draft in Gmail; never send without Joshua's explicit go-ahead.
- **Consultation reminder (day-of):**
  > Just checking in before our consultation today. Looking forward to speaking with you. I just wanted to make sure everything is still good.
- **Consultation follow-up:** warm, specific to their situation.
  > Really nice meeting you. I enjoyed our conversation ... I'm glad we got to talk through where you're at and what you're aiming for on test day.
- **Inbound prospect (hasn't booked):** state availability plainly, no overselling, hand them the next step.
  > Thanks for reaching out ... I'd be happy to talk more about what you're working on and see if we'd be a good fit. You can schedule a consultation here.
- **Student under real-life stress:** meet the person first.
  > Please don't apologize ... it sounds like you are dealing with an incredibly stressful and demanding situation.

## The three registers

Which voice governs which surface.

---
name: ai-os-safety-rules
description: In Joshua's AI OS, send/delete are blocked at the tool layer, not just by prompt � don't work around it.
metadata:
  type: feedback
---

`.claude/settings.json` in the AI OS vault denies Gmail send/delete/trash and Drive delete outright, alongside the "confirm first" rule in `CLAUDE.md`.

**Why:** A prompt is a suggestion, not a permission layer � an agent that obeys an instruction 100 times can ignore it on the 101st. Google's OAuth grant is genuinely broad (it includes deleting all Drive files), so the constraint has to live in the tool layer, not the wording.

**How to apply:** Draft, never send. If a task seems to need sending or deleting, ask Joshua rather than editing the deny list. If a grant is genuinely needed, narrow it to the single case instead of removing the deny. Related: [[ai-os-vault-location]]

## assistant

Here is where we are, in plain steps.

**The problem**

1. Sandra has a Booking Pass. It has 10 sessions. She opens her link and sees a list of Slots.
2. She picked a Slot and pressed Book. The app said "That time was just taken."
3. Nothing was taken. No booking was made. Her Booking Pass still has 10 sessions.
4. The cause: two parts of the app used two different Buffers. The Slot list used 15 minutes. The database used 30 minutes. So the list showed Slots that the database then refused. 15 of the 109 Slots she saw were like this.

**The fix**

5. The Buffer is now 10 minutes. It is stored in one place. Every check reads that one value.
6. Slots now step 80 minutes apart (70-minute session plus 10-minute Buffer). Two Slots in a row can both be booked.
7. The Booking Window is now 45 days. Before, it was 21.
8. Date Overrides now exist. They copy what Cal.com does: hours for one date replace the weekly hours on that date.
9. Two old calendar rows blocked two Saturdays. They are removed. The calendar sync now removes such rows by itself.
10. Every refused booking is now written to a log, with the Slot and the reason.

**The proof**

11. I ran the new Slot list for Sandra's link. It gives 134 Slots. I sent all 134 to the database check. The database accepted all 134.
12. The code is merged and deployed. Sandra's link is the same as before and works now.

**The email**

13. I wrote a reply to Sandra. You edited it three times. I sent it when you said "send it."
14. The final text is saved in the voice file as the model for this type of email.

**The rule change**

15. Your global rule was "draft, never send." You changed it. New rule: draft by default; send only when you say "send" in chat. Delete rules did not change.

**Not done, by your choice**

16. Cal.com still uses a 0-minute Buffer. You said to leave Cal.com alone.

// The pass link's policy: the pass sets the length, the availability config
// sets everything else. Pitch is length + buffer so back-to-back slots are all
// bookable (80 minutes for the 70-minute session).
export function passSlotPolicy(pass: BookingPass, avail: BookingAvailability, nowMs: number, daysAhead?: number): SlotPolicy {
  const days = Math.min(Math.max(daysAhead ?? avail.windowDays, 1), avail.windowDays);
  let windowEndMs = nowMs + days * 86_400_000;
  if (pass.expires_at) {
    const exp = new Date(pass.expires_at).getTime();
    if (Number.isFinite(exp) && exp < windowEndMs) windowEndMs = exp;
  }
  return {
    lengthMin: pass.session_length_minutes,
    pitchMin: pass.session_length_minutes + avail.bufferMin,
    bufferBeforeMin: avail.bufferMin,
    bufferAfterMin: avail.bufferMin,
    leadMin: avail.leadMin,
    windowEndMs,

The single session moves to 45 calendar days,\",\"--     window_business_days. The single session moves to 8 calendar days,\")\nopen(p,'w',encoding='utf-8',newline='\\n').write(s)\nPYEOF\ncat > supabase/migrations/20260911020000_booking_window_8_days_by_date.sql <<'EOF'\n-- The Booking Window is 8 days on every link, counted in host-local calendar\n-- dates: today plus 8, through the end of that day. Joshua set the number on\n-- 2026-09-11 (Cal.com's session-link runs 45; he wants students booking close\n-- in). The pass RPC used to count 8 x 24 hours from \"now\", which dropped the\n-- last day's evening slots that the single-session page still offered.\nUPDATE public.booking_availability_config SET window_days = 8, updated_at = now() WHERE id = 1;\nUPDATE public.booking_event_types SET window_calendar_days = 8, updated_at = now()\nWHERE slug IN ('single-session', 'consultation');\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_slot_bookable(_start_at timestamptz, _len integer)\nRETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _cfg        public.booking_availability_config%ROWTYPE;\n  _tz         text;\n  _buffer     integer;\n  _pitch      integer;\n  _local      timestamp;\n  _local_date date;\n  _local_time time;\n  _today      date;\n  _r          record;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _cfg FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1;\n  _tz     := COALESCE(_cfg.timezone, 'America/Toronto');\n  _buffer := COALESCE(_cfg.buffer_minutes, 10);\n  _pitch  := _len + _buffer;\n\n  IF _start_at IS NULL OR _len IS NULL OR _len <= 0 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF EXTRACT(SECOND FROM _start_at)::int <> 0 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF _start_at < now() + make_interval(mins => COALESCE(_cfg.minimum_notice_minutes, 1440)) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  _local      := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;\n  _local_date := _local::date;\n  _local_time := _local::time;\n  _today      := (now() AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date;\n\n  -- Window in calendar dates, the same count the single-session page and\n  -- Cal.com use: today + window_days is the last bookable date.\n  IF _local_date > _today + COALESCE(_cfg.window_days, 8) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  FOR _r IN SELECT * FROM public.booking_ranges_for_date(_local_date) LOOP\n    IF _local_time >= _r.start_time\n       AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _len) <= _r.end_time\n       AND mod(floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - _r.start_time)) / 60)::integer, _pitch) = 0\n    THEN\n      RETURN true;\n    END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  RETURN false;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND;\n$function$;\nEOF\necho written", "description": "Write the follow-up migration for the date-counted 8-day window"}

{"project_id": "53b9a6f2-aba7-4ae4-8dd1-fa5b8aab4d76", "sql": "BEGIN;\nUPDATE public.booking_availability_config SET window_days = 8, updated_at = now() WHERE id = 1;\nUPDATE public.booking_event_types SET window_calendar_days = 8, updated_at = now()\nWHERE slug IN ('single-session', 'consultation');\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_slot_bookable(_start_at timestamptz, _len integer)\nRETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nDECLARE\n  _cfg        public.booking_availability_config%ROWTYPE;\n  _tz         text;\n  _buffer     integer;\n  _pitch      integer;\n  _local      timestamp;\n  _local_date date;\n  _local_time time;\n  _today      date;\n  _r          record;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _cfg FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1;\n  _tz     := COALESCE(_cfg.timezone, 'America/Toronto');\n  _buffer := COALESCE(_cfg.buffer_minutes, 10);\n  _pitch  := _len + _buffer;\n\n  IF _start_at IS NULL OR _len IS NULL OR _len <= 0 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF EXTRACT(SECOND FROM _start_at)::int <> 0 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF _start_at < now() + make_interval(mins => COALESCE(_cfg.minimum_notice_minutes, 1440)) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  _local      := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;\n  _local_date := _local::date;\n  _local_time := _local::time;\n  _today      := (now() AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date;\n\n  IF _local_date > _today + COALESCE(_cfg.window_days, 8) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  FOR _r IN SELECT * FROM public.booking_ranges_for_date(_local_date) LOOP\n    IF _local_time >= _r.start_time\n       AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _len) <= _r.end_time\n       AND mod(floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - _r.start_time)) / 60)::integer, _pitch) = 0\n    THEN\n      RETURN true;\n    END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  RETURN false;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND;\n$function$;\nCOMMIT;\nSELECT public.is_slot_bookable('2026-09-20 00:00:00+00'::timestamptz, 70) AS sat_19_2000_et_bookable,\n       public.is_slot_bookable('2026-09-21 18:00:00+00'::timestamptz, 70) AS mon_21_outside_window;"}
